Implement the following plan:

# Plan: Fix Grounding Failures

## Context

The protocol processing pipeline (ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist) has 6 documented grounding failures that cause entities to fail terminology matching. Root causes span prompt engineering, schema validation, domain filtering, and acronym handling. This plan addresses failures #1-#6 from `instructions/SubmissionRequirements/grounding_failures/fixing_failing_groundings.md` (failure #7 is a frontend UI issue, out of scope for this backend plan).

---

## Fix 1: Over-Decomposition of Complex Concepts

**Problem**: LLM splits compound medical concepts ("tricompartmental knee replacement" → "knee replacement" + orphaned "tricompartmental"). Conversely, multi-condition phrases sent as one entity get zero matches.

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/entity_decompose.jinja2`

**Changes**:
- Add explicit rules to the Jinja2 prompt:
  - "Do NOT separate anatomical modifiers, severities, or procedure types from their parent concept. Keep 'tricompartmental knee replacement', 'spinal muscular atrophy', 'non-small cell lung cancer' as single entities."
  - "DO separate distinct comorbidities into individual entities. 'Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy' → two entities: 'type 2 diabetes' AND 'nephropathy'."
  - "The `value` field is NOT used by this tool. Only extract `text` (the medical concept) and `entity_type`."
- Add concrete positive/negative examples to the prompt.

---

## Fix 2: Hallucinated Relations in Field Mappings

**Problem**: `FieldMappingItem.relation` is an unconstrained `str`. LLM generates invalid operators like "contains", "has", "is".

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`

**Changes**:
- Change `FieldMappingItem.relation` from `str` to `Literal["=", "!=", ">", ">=", "<", "<=", "within", "not_in_last", "contains", "not_contains"]`
- Add a `@field_validator("relation", mode="before")` that runs `_normalize_relation()` before validation, so LLM outputs like "has", "is", "==" get normalized to valid values before the `Literal` check rejects them
- This leverages Gemini's `with_structured_output` retry-on-ValidationError behavior

---

## Fix 3: Categorical Values Not Grounded to Terminology

**Problem**: Field mapping values like "Severe", "NYHA Class III", "Stage IV" are left as raw text without terminology codes.

**Files**:
- `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`
- `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

**Changes**:
- In `field_mapper.py`, add `value_concept_id: str | None` and `value_concept_system: str | None` fields to `FieldMappingItem`
- In `ground.py` `_ground_entity_parallel()`, after generating field_mappings, add a post-processing step that:
  1. Checks each mapping's value — if it's `type="standard"` and the value text is non-numeric (regex check), attempt to ground it
  2. Uses the existing `unit_normalizer.normalize_value()` first (fast path for known categoricals like "positive"/"negative")
  3. Falls back to a lightweight `lookup_omop_concept()` call for clinical text values
  4. Populates `value_concept_id` on the mapping dict
- Guard with a try/except so failures don't block the entity grounding result

---

## Fix 4: Retry Loop Wastes Steps on Identical Queries

**Problem**: When initial grounding returns zero candidates, the agentic reasoning loop retries with near-identical queries, wasting all 3 attempts.

**Files**:
- `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/grounding_system.jinja2`
- `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/grounding_reasoning.jinja2`

**Changes to `grounding_system.jinja2`**:
- Add a "Search Strategy" section:
  - "If your first terminology search yields 0 results, DO NOT retry the exact same phrase or minor variations."
  - "Simplify to the core medical noun: 'Left ventricular hypertrophy' → 'ventricular hypertrophy' → 'hypertrophy'."
  - "Expand abbreviations: 'HTN' → 'hypertension', 'MI' → 'myocardial infarction'."

**Changes to `grounding_reasoning.jinja2`**:
- In Q3 (Rephrase), add explicit instruction: "If the previous search returned 0 results, you MUST suggest a substantially different, simpler query — not a minor variation of the same phrase."
- Add examples of good simplifications.

---

## Fix 5: Overly Restrictive OMOP Domain Filtering

**Problem**: `omop_mapper.py` filters by a single OMOP domain per entity type. "Pregnancy" (Observation) searched only in "Condition" gets zero results.

**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`

**Changes**:
- Replace `_get_domain_filter() -> str` with `_get_domain_filters() -> list[str]` that returns primary + fallback domains:
  ```python
  ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAINS: dict[str, list[str]] = {
      "Condition": ["Condition", "Observation"],
      "Medication": ["Drug"],
      "Lab_Value": ["Measurement", "Observation"],
      "Procedure": ["Procedure", "Observation"],
      "Demographic": ["Observation", "Measurement"],
  }
  ```
- Update `_query_concept_table()` and `_query_synonym_table()` SQL to use `domain_id IN :domains` instead of `domain_id = :domain_id`
- Update `_sync_lookup()` to pass the domain list
- Score candidates from the primary domain higher (small bonus) to prefer correct-domain matches

---

## Fix 6: Tools-First Acronym Resolution (Zero Extra LLM Tokens)

**Problem**: Terminology APIs don't recognize medical acronyms ("HTN", "MI", "DM", "CKD"). The TerminologyRouter sends them raw and gets zero results.

**Approach**: Use existing terminology tools (UMLS, SNOMED, OMOP concept_synonym) smarter — they already index abbreviations. Only fall back to MedGemma's agentic retry loop (Fix 4) if all tools fail. Zero additional LLM token cost.

**Files**:
- `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/terminology_router.py`
- `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`
- `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

**Changes**:

1. **Add `_is_likely_acronym(text: str) -> bool` heuristic** in `terminology_router.py`:
   - Returns True if: `len(text) <= 6` AND `text.upper() == text` AND no spaces AND not a known short word (allowlist: "age", "sex", "BMI")
   - Pure string check, zero cost

2. **Smart routing for acronyms** in `terminology_router.py` `route_entity()`:
   - If `_is_likely_acronym(entity_text)`, always include `umls` in the API list regardless of entity type (UMLS has the broadest abbreviation coverage via its AUI atom-level synonyms)
   - Log: "Acronym detected — adding UMLS to routing for broader abbreviation coverage"
   - No extra LLM call — just ensures the most abbreviation-aware API is always queried

3. **Domain-free OMOP synonym search for acronyms** in `omop_mapper.py`:
   - If entity text is a likely acronym, skip the domain filter entirely in `_query_synonym_table()` (search across ALL domains)
   - The CONCEPT_SYNONYM table contains standard abbreviations — this finds them regardless of which domain the entity type maps to
   - Add `is_acronym: bool = False` parameter to `_sync_lookup()` and `lookup_omop_concept()`

4. **MedGemma as last resort** (already existing):
   - If all terminology tools return zero candidates for the acronym, the existing `_ground_entity_with_retry()` loop triggers `agentic_reasoning_loop()` (max 3 attempts)
   - The improved prompts from Fix 4 now explicitly guide MedGemma to expand abbreviations in Q3
   - This is the only point where LLM tokens are spent — and only for acronyms that ALL tools failed to resolve

---

## Tests

### New test file: `services/protocol-processor-service/tests/test_grounding_fixes.py`

1. **test_relation_literal_rejects_invalid** — Verify `FieldMappingItem(relation="has_value")` raises `ValidationError`
2. **test_relation_normalizer_maps_legacy** — Verify "has"→"contains", "is"→"=", "=="→"="
3. **test_is_likely_acronym** — Verify heuristic detects "HTN", "MI", "CKD", "COPD" as acronyms but not "age", "metformin", "body mass index", "sex"
4. **test_acronym_routing_adds_umls** — Verify that for acronyms, `umls` is always added to the API list even if not in the entity type's routing config
5. **test_omop_acronym_drops_domain_filter** — Verify that `lookup_omop_concept` with `is_acronym=True` searches across all domains in concept_synonym
5. **test_omop_multi_domain_query** — Verify OMOP queries search multiple domains (mock SQL)
6. **test_omop_primary_domain_scored_higher** — Verify primary domain candidates get score bonus
7. **test_decompose_keeps_compound_concepts** — Integration test with Gemini mock: verify "tricompartmental knee replacement" stays unified
8. **test_decompose_splits_comorbidities** — Verify "Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy" produces 2 entities

### Update existing tests:
- `test_field_mapper_format.py` — add cases for new Literal relation validation
- `test_unit_normalizer.py` — no changes needed (already tests normalize_value)
- `test_omop_mapper.py` — update to expect multi-domain queries

---

```

### Manual validation with known-failing cases:
Create a lightweight validation script `scripts/validate_grounding_fixes.py` that:
1. Constructs entity dicts for each documented failure case
2. Verifies acronym expansion works (HTN, MI, DM, CKD → expanded forms)
3. Verifies relation validation rejects hallucinated operators
4. Verifies multi-domain OMOP query construction (SQL template check)
5. Verifies decomposition prompt contains anti-decomposition rules (string check)
6. Prints PASS/FAIL summary

     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any, Literal
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    21→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    22→    create_structured_llm,
    23→    parse_structured_output,
    24→)
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Mapping from legacy/LLM relation operators to the frontend's RelationOperator set
    29→_RELATION_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    30→    "has": "contains",
    31→    "is": "=",
    32→    "not": "not_contains",
    33→    "==": "=",
    34→    "range": "within",
    35→}
    36→
    37→
    38→def _normalize_relation(rel: str) -> str:
    39→    """Normalize a relation operator to the frontend's accepted set.
    40→
    41→    Maps legacy operators (has, is, not, ==, range) to the standard set:
    42→    =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains.
    43→    """
    44→    return _RELATION_MAP.get(rel, rel)
    45→
    46→
    47→class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    48→    """Typed value object for field mappings with type discriminator.
    49→
    50→    Supports three value shapes:
    51→    - standard: single value + unit (e.g. HbA1c < 7%)
    52→    - range: min/max + unit (e.g. Age 18-65 years)
    53→    - temporal: duration + unit (e.g. within 6 months)
    54→    """
    55→
    56→    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"] = Field(
    57→        description="Value type discriminator"
    58→    )
    59→    value: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None, description="Value for standard type (e.g. '7')"
    61→    )
    62→    unit: str | None = Field(
    63→        default=None, description="Unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'months')"
    64→    )
    65→    min: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Minimum value for range type")
    66→    max: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Maximum value for range type")
    67→    duration: str | None = Field(
    68→        default=None, description="Duration value for temporal type"
    69→    )
    70→
    71→
    72→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    73→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    74→
    75→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
    76→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
    77→    """
    78→
    79→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
    80→    relation: str = Field(
    81→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')"
    82→    )
    83→    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(
    84→        description="Typed value object with type discriminator"
    85→    )
    86→    unit: str | None = Field(
    87→        default=None,
    88→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
    89→    )
    90→
    91→
    92→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    93→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
    94→
    95→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
    96→        default_factory=list,
    97→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    98→    )
    99→
   100→
   101→async def generate_field_mappings(
   102→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
   103→    criterion_text: str,
   104→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   105→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
   106→
   107→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
   108→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
   109→
   110→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
   111→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
   112→
   113→    Args:
   114→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
   115→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   116→
   117→    Returns:
   118→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value,
   119→        entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type.
   120→        Empty list if generation fails.
   121→    """
   122→    if not criterion_text:
   123→        return []
   124→
   125→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
   126→    if structured_llm is None:
   127→        return []
   128→
   129→    try:
   130→        # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
   131→        grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   132→        code_context = ""
   133→        if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   134→            system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   135→            code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   136→
   137→        prompt = (
   138→            "You are a clinical trial protocol analyst. Decompose the"
   139→            " following criterion into structured AutoCriteria field"
   140→            " mappings using the Entity-Relation-Value-Unit pattern.\n\n"
   141→            f"Medical entity: {grounded_term} {code_context}\n"
   142→            f"Criterion text: {criterion_text}\n\n"
   143→            "Instructions:\n"
   144→            "- Extract each discrete measurement, threshold, or condition"
   145→            " as a separate mapping\n"
   146→            "- entity: the specific measurement or concept"
   147→            " (e.g. 'HbA1c', 'Age', 'eGFR')\n"
   148→            "- relation: MUST be one of: =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within,"
   149→            " not_in_last, contains, not_contains\n"
   150→            '- value: a typed object with a "type" discriminator:\n'
   151→            '  - Standard: {"type": "standard", "value": "7", "unit": "%"}\n'
   152→            '  - Range: {"type": "range", "min": "18", "max": "65",'
   153→            ' "unit": "years"}\n'
   154→            '  - Temporal: {"type": "temporal", "duration": "6",'
   155→            ' "unit": "months"}\n'
   156→            "- Create one mapping per discrete condition in the criterion\n"
   157→            "- If no clear measurement exists, create one mapping with"
   158→            " relation='contains',"
   159→            ' value={"type": "standard", "value": "confirmed", "unit": ""}'
   160→        )
   161→
   162→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   163→        response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   164→
   165→        mappings = [
   166→            {
   167→                "entity": m.entity,
   168→                "relation": _normalize_relation(m.relation),
   169→                "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
   170→                "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
   171→                "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
   172→                "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   173→                "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   174→            }
   175→            for m in response.mappings
   176→        ]
   177→
   178→        logger.info(
   179→            "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   180→            len(mappings),
   181→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   182→        )
   183→        return mappings
   184→
   185→    except Exception as e:
   186→        logger.warning(
   187→            "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s': %s",
   188→            entity.entity_text[:50],
   189→            e,
   190→            exc_info=True,
   191→        )
   192→        # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   193→        return []
   194→

     1→"""OMOP vocabulary mapper: look up standard OMOP concept_ids for medical entities.
     2→
     3→Queries Athena vocabulary tables (concept, concept_synonym) in a dedicated
     4→OMOP vocabulary database. Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable
     5→pointing to a Postgres instance with loaded Athena vocabulary data.
     6→
     7→This tool provides the OMOP grounding leg of the dual-grounding pipeline:
     8→- TerminologyRouter provides UMLS/SNOMED/RxNorm/ICD-10/LOINC/HPO codes
     9→- OmopMapper provides OMOP standard concept_ids for CIRCE export and CDM joins
    10→
    11→Connection: Uses a dedicated SQLAlchemy engine created from OMOP_VOCAB_URL.
    12→This is separate from the main app database (DATABASE_URL). If OMOP_VOCAB_URL
    13→is not set, lookup_omop_concept raises RuntimeError — callers must handle this
    14→explicitly rather than silently receiving empty results.
    15→
    16→All DB I/O is synchronous SQLAlchemy wrapped in run_in_executor for async
    17→compatibility.
    18→"""
    19→
    20→from __future__ import annotations
    21→
    22→import asyncio
    23→import logging
    24→import os
    25→from difflib import SequenceMatcher
    26→from typing import Any
    27→
    28→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    29→from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    30→
    31→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    32→
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Configuration
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE: float = 0.3
    38→"""Minimum fuzzy-match score to accept a candidate. Below this, return None."""
    39→
    40→OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES: int = 50
    41→"""Maximum number of rows to fetch from each SQL query (LIMIT clause)."""
    42→
    43→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    44→# Dedicated OMOP vocabulary engine (lazy singleton)
    45→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    46→
    47→_omop_engine: Any = None
    48→
    49→
    50→def _get_omop_engine() -> Any:
    51→    """Return the OMOP vocabulary SQLAlchemy engine.
    52→
    53→    Creates a dedicated engine from OMOP_VOCAB_URL on first call.
    54→    Raises RuntimeError if the env var is not set — callers must
    55→    handle this rather than silently degrading.
    56→    """
    57→    global _omop_engine  # noqa: PLW0603
    58→    if _omop_engine is not None:
    59→        return _omop_engine
    60→
    61→    omop_url = os.getenv("OMOP_VOCAB_URL")
    62→    if not omop_url:
    63→        raise RuntimeError(
    64→            "OMOP_VOCAB_URL environment variable is not set. "
    65→            "Set it to the OMOP vocabulary Postgres connection string "
    66→            "(e.g. postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5433/omop_vocab) "
    67→            "or start the omop-vocab container: "
    68→            "docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml --profile omop up -d"
    69→        )
    70→
    71→    _omop_engine = create_engine(omop_url, pool_pre_ping=True)
    72→    logger.info("OMOP vocabulary engine created: %s", omop_url.split("@")[-1])
    73→    return _omop_engine
    74→
    75→
    76→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    77→# Entity type -> OMOP domain mapping
    78→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    79→
    80→ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN: dict[str, str] = {
    81→    "Condition": "Condition",
    82→    "Medication": "Drug",
    83→    "Lab_Value": "Measurement",
    84→    "Procedure": "Procedure",
    85→    "Demographic": "Observation",
    86→}
    87→"""Map pipeline entity types to OMOP CDM domain_id values.
    88→
    89→Demographic is a catch-all routed to Observation. Entity types not present
    90→here will fall back to Observation as well.
    91→"""
    92→
    93→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    94→# Result schema
    95→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    96→
    97→
    98→class OmopLookupResult(BaseModel):
    99→    """Result of an OMOP vocabulary lookup for a single entity.
   100→
   101→    Attributes:
   102→        omop_concept_id: Matched OMOP concept_id as a string, or None.
   103→        omop_concept_name: Matched concept_name from the vocabulary, or None.
   104→        omop_vocabulary_id: Vocabulary source of the match (e.g. SNOMED, RxNorm).
   105→        omop_domain_id: OMOP domain_id of the matched concept.
   106→        match_score: Fuzzy similarity score between 0.0 and 1.0.
   107→        match_method: How the match was found: "concept_name" or "synonym".
   108→    """
   109→
   110→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   111→        default=None,
   112→        description=("Matched OMOP concept_id as a string, or None if no match."),
   113→    )
   114→    omop_concept_name: str | None = Field(
   115→        default=None,
   116→        description="Matched concept_name from the OMOP vocabulary.",
   117→    )
   118→    omop_vocabulary_id: str | None = Field(
   119→        default=None,
   120→        description=("Vocabulary source of the match (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'RxNorm')."),
   121→    )
   122→    omop_domain_id: str | None = Field(
   123→        default=None,
   124→        description="OMOP domain_id of the matched concept.",
   125→    )
   126→    match_score: float = Field(
   127→        default=0.0,
   128→        ge=0.0,
   129→        le=1.0,
   130→        description="Fuzzy similarity score between 0.0 and 1.0.",
   131→    )
   132→    match_method: str = Field(
   133→        default="",
   134→        description='How the match was found: "concept_name" or "synonym".',
   135→    )
   136→
   137→
   138→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   139→# Internal helpers
   140→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   141→
   142→
   143→def _score_candidates(
   144→    entity_text: str, candidates: list[dict[str, Any]]
   145→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   146→    """Score and sort candidates by fuzzy string similarity to *entity_text*.
   147→
   148→    Uses ``difflib.SequenceMatcher`` ratio as the base score, with a bonus
   149→    for exact case-insensitive substring containment.
   150→
   151→    Args:
   152→        entity_text: The original entity text to match against.
   153→        candidates: List of candidate dicts, each with at least a
   154→            ``"match_text"`` key containing the string to compare.
   155→
   156→    Returns:
   157→        The same candidate dicts augmented with a ``"score"`` key, sorted
   158→        in descending order of score.
   159→    """
   160→    entity_lower = entity_text.lower().strip()
   161→
   162→    for candidate in candidates:
   163→        match_text = candidate.get("match_text", "").lower().strip()
   164→
   165→        # Base score: SequenceMatcher ratio
   166→        base_score = SequenceMatcher(None, entity_lower, match_text).ratio()
   167→
   168→        # Bonus for exact substring containment (either direction)
   169→        bonus = 0.0
   170→        if entity_lower in match_text or match_text in entity_lower:
   171→            bonus = 0.15
   172→
   173→        # Bonus for exact match
   174→        if entity_lower == match_text:
   175→            bonus = 0.25
   176→
   177→        candidate["score"] = min(base_score + bonus, 1.0)
   178→
   179→    # Sort by score desc, then by length similarity (prefer match_text
   180→    # closest in length to entity_text) as tiebreaker.
   181→    entity_len = len(entity_lower)
   182→
   183→    def _sort_key(c: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[float, int]:
   184→        match_len = len(c.get("match_text", "").strip())
   185→        return (c["score"], -abs(match_len - entity_len))
   186→
   187→    candidates.sort(key=_sort_key, reverse=True)
   188→    return candidates
   189→
   190→
   191→def _get_domain_filter(entity_type: str) -> str:
   192→    """Resolve entity type to an OMOP domain_id filter value.
   193→
   194→    Falls back to ``"Observation"`` for unknown entity types.
   195→
   196→    Args:
   197→        entity_type: Pipeline entity type (e.g. "Condition", "Medication").
   198→
   199→    Returns:
   200→        OMOP domain_id string.
   201→    """
   202→    domain = ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN.get(entity_type, "Observation")
   203→    if entity_type not in ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN:
   204→        logger.info(
   205→            "Entity type '%s' not in OMOP domain map — defaulting to '%s'",
   206→            entity_type,
   207→            domain,
   208→        )
   209→    return domain
   210→
   211→
   212→def _query_concept_table(
   213→    engine: Any, entity_text: str, domain_id: str
   214→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   215→    """Query concept table for standard concepts matching *entity_text*.
   216→
   217→    Args:
   218→        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.
   219→        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).
   220→        domain_id: OMOP domain_id filter.
   221→
   222→    Returns:
   223→        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,
   224→        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.
   225→    """
   226→    sql = text(
   227→        "SELECT concept_id, concept_name, domain_id, vocabulary_id "
   228→        "FROM concept "
   229→        "WHERE standard_concept = 'S' "
   230→        "  AND domain_id = :domain_id "
   231→        "  AND concept_name ILIKE :pattern "
   232→        "ORDER BY length(concept_name) ASC "
   233→        "LIMIT :max_candidates"
   234→    )
   235→    pattern = f"%{entity_text}%"
   236→    with engine.connect() as conn:
   237→        rows = conn.execute(
   238→            sql,
   239→            {
   240→                "domain_id": domain_id,
   241→                "pattern": pattern,
   242→                "max_candidates": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,
   243→            },
   244→        ).fetchall()
   245→
   246→    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   247→    for row in rows:
   248→        candidates.append(
   249→            {
   250→                "concept_id": str(row[0]),
   251→                "concept_name": row[1],
   252→                "domain_id": row[2],
   253→                "vocabulary_id": row[3],
   254→                "match_text": row[1],
   255→                "match_method": "concept_name",
   256→            }
   257→        )
   258→    return candidates
   259→
   260→
   261→def _query_synonym_table(
   262→    engine: Any, entity_text: str, domain_id: str
   263→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   264→    """Query concept_synonym joined with concept for synonym matches.
   265→
   266→    Args:
   267→        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.
   268→        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).
   269→        domain_id: OMOP domain_id filter.
   270→
   271→    Returns:
   272→        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,
   273→        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.
   274→    """
   275→    sql = text(
   276→        "SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name, c.domain_id, "
   277→        "       c.vocabulary_id, s.concept_synonym_name "
   278→        "FROM concept_synonym s "
   279→        "JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id "
   280→        "WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' "
   281→        "  AND c.domain_id = :domain_id "
   282→        "  AND s.concept_synonym_name ILIKE :pattern "
   283→        "ORDER BY length(s.concept_synonym_name) ASC "
   284→        "LIMIT :max_candidates"
   285→    )
   286→    pattern = f"%{entity_text}%"
   287→    with engine.connect() as conn:
   288→        rows = conn.execute(
   289→            sql,
   290→            {
   291→                "domain_id": domain_id,
   292→                "pattern": pattern,
   293→                "max_candidates": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,
   294→            },
   295→        ).fetchall()
   296→
   297→    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   298→    for row in rows:
   299→        candidates.append(
   300→            {
   301→                "concept_id": str(row[0]),
   302→                "concept_name": row[1],
   303→                "domain_id": row[2],
   304→                "vocabulary_id": row[3],
   305→                "match_text": row[4],  # synonym name used for scoring
   306→                "match_method": "synonym",
   307→            }
   308→        )
   309→    return candidates
   310→
   311→
   312→def _sync_lookup(entity_text: str, domain_id: str) -> OmopLookupResult:
   313→    """Synchronous OMOP lookup — runs inside run_in_executor.
   314→
   315→    Queries both concept and concept_synonym tables, deduplicates
   316→    candidates by concept_id (preferring concept_name match), scores them,
   317→    and returns the best match above the threshold.
   318→
   319→    Args:
   320→        entity_text: The entity text to look up.
   321→        domain_id: OMOP domain_id to filter on.
   322→
   323→    Returns:
   324→        OmopLookupResult with best match, or empty result if nothing found
   325→        or all candidates score below OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE.
   326→
   327→    Raises:
   328→        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not configured.
   329→        Exception: Database errors are propagated, not swallowed.
   330→    """
   331→    engine = _get_omop_engine()
   332→
   333→    concept_candidates = _query_concept_table(engine, entity_text, domain_id)
   334→    synonym_candidates = _query_synonym_table(engine, entity_text, domain_id)
   335→
   336→    # Deduplicate by concept_id — prefer concept_name match over synonym
   337→    seen: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
   338→    for candidate in concept_candidates:
   339→        seen[candidate["concept_id"]] = candidate
   340→    for candidate in synonym_candidates:
   341→        if candidate["concept_id"] not in seen:
   342→            seen[candidate["concept_id"]] = candidate
   343→
   344→    all_candidates = list(seen.values())
   345→
   346→    if not all_candidates:
   347→        logger.debug(
   348→            "No OMOP candidates found for '%s' in domain '%s'",
   349→            entity_text[:50],
   350→            domain_id,
   351→        )
   352→        return OmopLookupResult()
   353→
   354→    # Score and sort
   355→    scored = _score_candidates(entity_text, all_candidates)
   356→    best = scored[0]
   357→
   358→    if best["score"] < OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE:
   359→        logger.debug(
   360→            "Best OMOP candidate for '%s' scored %.3f "
   361→            "(below threshold %.2f) — skipping",
   362→            entity_text[:50],
   363→            best["score"],
   364→            OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE,
   365→        )
   366→        return OmopLookupResult()
   367→
   368→    logger.info(
   369→        "OMOP match for '%s': concept_id=%s, name='%s', score=%.3f, method=%s",
   370→        entity_text[:50],
   371→        best["concept_id"],
   372→        best["concept_name"][:60],
   373→        best["score"],
   374→        best["match_method"],
   375→    )
   376→
   377→    return OmopLookupResult(
   378→        omop_concept_id=best["concept_id"],
   379→        omop_concept_name=best["concept_name"],
   380→        omop_vocabulary_id=best["vocabulary_id"],
   381→        omop_domain_id=best["domain_id"],
   382→        match_score=best["score"],
   383→        match_method=best["match_method"],
   384→    )
   385→
   386→
   387→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   388→# Public async API
   389→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   390→
   391→
   392→async def lookup_omop_concept(entity_text: str, entity_type: str) -> OmopLookupResult:
   393→    """Look up the best OMOP standard concept for a medical entity.
   394→
   395→    Maps the entity type to an OMOP domain filter, queries the concept
   396→    and concept_synonym tables in the OMOP vocabulary database, scores
   397→    candidates with fuzzy string matching, and returns the best result
   398→    above the minimum similarity threshold.
   399→
   400→    The synchronous database I/O is executed in a thread-pool executor to
   401→    avoid blocking the async event loop.
   402→
   403→    Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL to be set. Raises RuntimeError if not configured.
   404→
   405→    Args:
   406→        entity_text: The medical entity text to look up (e.g. "metformin",
   407→            "type 2 diabetes mellitus", "hemoglobin A1c").
   408→        entity_type: Pipeline entity type used to determine the OMOP domain
   409→            filter. One of "Condition", "Medication", "Lab_Value",
   410→            "Procedure", "Demographic". Unknown types fall back to
   411→            "Observation".
   412→
   413→    Returns:
   414→        OmopLookupResult with the best-matching OMOP concept, or an empty
   415→        result if no match is found above the similarity threshold.
   416→
   417→    Raises:
   418→        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not set.
   419→    """
   420→    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():
   421→        raise ValueError("Empty entity_text passed to lookup_omop_concept")
   422→
   423→    domain_id = _get_domain_filter(entity_type)
   424→
   425→    logger.debug(
   426→        "OMOP lookup: entity='%s', type='%s', domain='%s'",
   427→        entity_text[:50],
   428→        entity_type,
   429→        domain_id,
   430→    )
   431→
   432→    result = await asyncio.to_thread(_sync_lookup, entity_text, domain_id)
   433→
   434→    return result
   435→
```

     1→"""TerminologyRouter: entity-type-aware routing to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse.
     2→
     3→Routes entities to terminology APIs based on entity type using a YAML config.
     4→All 6 systems (UMLS, SNOMED, ICD-10, LOINC, RxNorm, HPO) are accessed via the
     5→ToolUniverse SDK singleton in tooluniverse_client.py.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Entity type → API mapping stored in config file (YAML), not hardcoded.
     9→- All terminology systems accessed via ToolUniverse SDK (single dependency).
    10→- On API failure: retry transient errors; continue on permanent failures.
    11→- Consent entities explicitly skipped with logging.
    12→- Demographic entities routed normally (not skipped); MedGemma handles derived
    13→  entity mapping (age → birthDate concept, gender → SNOMED gender concepts).
    14→
    15→See config/routing.yaml for the entity type → API mapping.
    16→"""

    26→    before_sleep_log,
    27→    retry,

    34→

    36→

    39→
    40→# diskcache TTL: 7 days in seconds

    42→
    43→
    44→def _get_cache():
    45→    """Get or create the diskcache Cache instance.
    46→
    47→    Returns:
    48→        diskcache.Cache instance stored in user cache dir, or None if

    50→    """

    53→
    54→        cache_dir = Path(user_cache_dir("medgemma-terminology-router"))
    55→        return diskcache.Cache(str(cache_dir))
    56→    except ImportError:
    57→        logger.debug("diskcache not installed — terminology API caching disabled.")
    58→        return None
    59→
    60→
    61→class TransientAPIError(Exception):
    62→    """Retriable API failure: rate limit (429), timeout (408), server error (5xx).
    63→
    64→    TerminologyRouter retries these automatically with exponential backoff.
    65→    """
    66→
    67→
    68→class PermanentAPIError(Exception):
    69→    """Non-retriable API failure: auth error (401/403), validation (400/422).
    70→
    71→    TerminologyRouter logs and skips these — retrying cannot fix them.
    72→    """
    73→
    74→
    75→class TerminologyRouter:
    76→    """Route entities to terminology APIs via ToolUniverse SDK.
    77→
    78→    Loads entity type → API mapping from routing.yaml. For each entity,
    79→    queries all matching APIs via the ToolUniverse singleton client and
    80→    returns a list of GroundingCandidate objects. MedGemma then selects
    81→    the best match downstream.
    82→
    83→    Implements error accumulation: individual API failures are logged and
    84→    skipped, not propagated. The entity is still grounded by remaining APIs.
    85→
    86→    Usage:
    87→        router = TerminologyRouter()
    88→        candidates = await router.route_entity("metformin", "Medication")
    89→        # candidates: [GroundingCandidate(source_api="rxnorm", ...), ...]
    90→
    91→    Attributes:
    92→        config: Parsed YAML config dict.
    93→        config_path: Path to the routing.yaml file used.
    94→    """
    95→
    96→    def __init__(self, config_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    97→        """Initialize TerminologyRouter with routing config.
    98→
    99→        Args:
   100→            config_path: Path to routing.yaml. Defaults to config/routing.yaml
   101→                relative to this package.
   102→        """
   103→        self.config_path = config_path or _DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH
   104→        with open(self.config_path) as f:
   105→            self.config: dict = yaml.safe_load(f)
   106→
   107→    def get_apis_for_entity(self, entity_type: str) -> list[str]:
   108→        """Get list of API names to query for this entity type.
   109→
   110→        Returns an empty list for entity types with skip: true in routing config
   111→        (e.g. Consent) and for entity types not found in the routing config.
   112→        Both cases are logged.
   113→
   114→        Args:
   115→            entity_type: Entity type string (e.g. "Medication", "Condition").
   116→
   117→        Returns:
   118→            List of API names to query, or empty list if entity should be skipped.
   119→        """
   120→        routing_rules: dict = self.config.get("routing_rules", {})
   121→        rule = routing_rules.get(entity_type)
   122→
   123→        if rule is None:
   124→            logger.warning(
   125→                "Unknown entity type '%s' — not in routing config. "
   126→                "Skipping grounding. Add to config/routing.yaml to enable.",
   127→                entity_type,
   128→            )
   129→            return []
   130→
   131→        # Explicit skip (e.g. Consent: {skip: true})
   132→        if isinstance(rule, dict) and rule.get("skip"):
   133→            logger.info(
   134→                "Entity type '%s' is explicitly skipped in routing config "
   135→                "(no terminology grounding for this type).",
   136→                entity_type,
   137→            )
   138→            return []
   139→
   140→        if isinstance(rule, list):
   141→            return rule
   142→
   143→        # Unexpected rule format — treat as unknown
   144→        logger.warning(
   145→            "Unexpected routing rule format for '%s': %r. Skipping.",
   146→            entity_type,
   147→            rule,
   148→        )
   149→        return []
   150→
   151→    async def route_entity(
   152→        self, entity_text: str, entity_type: str
   153→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   154→        """Route entity to all matching APIs and return all candidates.
   155→
   156→        Queries each API configured for the entity type via ToolUniverse SDK.
   157→        Individual API failures are logged and skipped (error accumulation).
   158→        The caller (MedGemma ground node) receives all available candidates
   159→        and selects the best match.
   160→
   161→        Args:
   162→            entity_text: The entity text to look up (e.g. "metformin").
   163→            entity_type: The entity type (e.g. "Medication").
   164→
   165→        Returns:
   166→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from all queried APIs.
   167→            Empty list if entity type is skipped or all APIs fail.
   168→        """
   169→        api_names = self.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   170→
   171→        if not api_names:
   172→            return []
   173→
   174→        all_candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = []
   175→
   176→        for api_name in api_names:
   177→            try:
   178→                candidates = await self._query_tooluniverse(api_name, entity_text)
   179→                all_candidates.extend(candidates)
   180→
   181→            except PermanentAPIError as e:
   182→                # Non-retriable: log and skip this API
   183→                logger.error(
   184→                    "Permanent API error from '%s' for entity '%s': %s",
   185→                    api_name,
   186→                    entity_text,
   187→                    e,
   188→                )
   189→            except Exception as e:
   190→                # Transient/unexpected: log and skip (tenacity already retried)
   191→                logger.error(
   192→                    "API '%s' failed for entity '%s' after retries: %s",
   193→                    api_name,
   194→                    entity_text,
   195→                    e,
   196→                )
   197→
   198→        return all_candidates
   199→
   200→    @retry(
   201→        retry=retry_if_exception_type(TransientAPIError),
   202→        stop=stop_after_attempt(3),
   203→        wait=wait_random_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
   204→        before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING),
   205→        reraise=True,
   206→    )
   207→    async def _query_tooluniverse(
   208→        self, api_name: str, entity_text: str
   209→    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
   210→        """Query terminology system via ToolUniverse client wrapper.
   211→
   212→        Uses the singleton search_terminology function which handles tool
   213→        dispatch, response parsing, and in-memory TTL caching.
   214→
   215→        Retries on TransientAPIError with exponential backoff (3 attempts max).
   216→
   217→        Args:
   218→            api_name: Terminology system name (e.g. "umls", "icd10").
   219→            entity_text: Term to search.
   220→
   221→        Returns:
   222→            List of GroundingCandidate objects from ToolUniverse.
   223→
   224→        Raises:
   225→            TransientAPIError: On retriable errors (rate limit, timeout, 5xx).
   226→        """
   227→        from protocol_processor.tools.tooluniverse_client import search_terminology
   228→
   229→        candidates = await search_terminology(api_name, entity_text, max_results=10)
   230→        return candidates
   231→

     1→"""Ground node: route entities via TerminologyRouter and select with MedGemma.
     2→
     3→This node is the fourth in the protocol processing pipeline:
     4→ingest→extract→parse→ground→persist
     5→It implements the delegation pattern: thin orchestration that calls tools.
     6→
     7→Per user decisions:
     8→- Error accumulation: entity failures are logged and accumulated, not fatal
     9→- Audit trail: all grounding decisions logged to AuditLog
    10→- TerminologyRouter handles entity-type-aware API routing
    11→- MedGemma selects the best match from candidates
    12→- Field mappings generated per entity for AutoCriteria decomposition
    13→- Consent entities skipped explicitly (not groundable to medical codes)
    14→- Demographics routed normally via umls/snomed (age/gender agentic reasoning)
    15→- Agentic retry loop: max 3 attempts, expert_review routing on exhaustion
    16→
    17→Architecture note: Graph nodes ARE allowed to import from api-service for DB access.
    18→"""

    21→

    27→from typing import Any

    46→from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter

    49→

    55→    """Get or create the singleton TerminologyRouter."""

    61→
    62→def _reconcile_dual_grounding(
    63→    result: EntityGroundingResult,
    64→    omop_result: OmopLookupResult,
    65→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    66→    """Reconcile TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper results.
    67→
    68→    Sets omop_concept_id and reconciliation_status on the result:
    69→    - Both succeed → "agree" (high confidence) or "disagreement"
    70→    - Only TerminologyRouter → "omop_missing"
    71→    - Only OMOP → "tooluniverse_missing"
    72→    - Neither → no change (existing expert_review behavior)
    73→
    74→    Agreement is checked by comparing the OMOP concept_name against
    75→    the ToolUniverse preferred_term (fuzzy match).
    76→
    77→    Args:
    78→        result: EntityGroundingResult from TerminologyRouter path.
    79→        omop_result: OmopLookupResult from OMOP mapper path.
    80→
    81→    Returns:
    82→        The same EntityGroundingResult, mutated with OMOP fields.
    83→    """
    84→    tu_ok = result.selected_code is not None
    85→    omop_ok = omop_result.omop_concept_id is not None
    86→
    87→    if tu_ok and omop_ok:
    88→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
    89→        # Fuzzy agreement check using token_set_ratio (order-insensitive)
    90→        tu_term = (result.preferred_term or "").lower()
    91→        omop_term = (omop_result.omop_concept_name or "").lower()
    92→        if tu_term and omop_term and fuzz.token_set_ratio(tu_term, omop_term) >= 80:
    93→            result.reconciliation_status = "agree"
    94→        else:
    95→            result.reconciliation_status = "disagreement"
    96→        logger.info(
    97→            "Dual grounding for '%s': TU=%s, OMOP=%s, status=%s",
    98→            result.entity_text[:50],
    99→            result.selected_code,
   100→            omop_result.omop_concept_id,
   101→            result.reconciliation_status,
   102→        )
   103→    elif tu_ok and not omop_ok:
   104→        result.reconciliation_status = "omop_missing"
   105→    elif not tu_ok and omop_ok:
   106→        result.omop_concept_id = omop_result.omop_concept_id
   107→        result.reconciliation_status = "tooluniverse_missing"
   108→    # else: neither — leave as-is (expert_review path)
   109→
   110→    return result
   111→
   112→
   113→def _log_grounding_audit(
   114→    session: Session,
   115→    protocol_id: str,
   116→    criterion_id: str,
   117→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   118→    candidates: list[Any],
   119→    result: Any,
   120→) -> None:
   121→    """Create an AuditLog entry for a grounding decision.
   122→
   123→    Records full details: entity, all candidates considered, selected code,
   124→    reasoning, and confidence. Provides the audit trail per user decision:
   125→    "All agent exchanges (MedGemma decisions, API calls, routing) are logged."
   126→
   127→    Args:
   128→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   129→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   130→        criterion_id: Criterion ID this entity belongs to.
   131→        entity: Entity dict from entities_json.
   132→        candidates: GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   133→        result: EntityGroundingResult from medgemma_decide.
   134→    """
   135→    audit = AuditLog(
   136→        event_type="entity_grounded",
   137→        target_type="criteria",
   138→        target_id=criterion_id,
   139→        details={
   140→            "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   141→            "entity_text": result.entity_text,
   142→            "entity_type": result.entity_type,
   143→            "candidate_count": len(candidates),
   144→            "candidates": [
   145→                {
   146→                    "source_api": c.source_api,
   147→                    "code": c.code,
   148→                    "preferred_term": c.preferred_term,
   149→                    "score": c.score,
   150→                }
   151→                for c in candidates
   152→            ],
   153→            "selected_code": result.selected_code,
   154→            "selected_system": result.selected_system,
   155→            "preferred_term": result.preferred_term,
   156→            "confidence": result.confidence,
   157→            "reasoning": result.reasoning,
   158→            "omop_concept_id": result.omop_concept_id,
   159→            "reconciliation_status": result.reconciliation_status,
   160→        },
   161→    )
   162→    session.add(audit)
   163→
   164→
   165→def _flush_audit_logs(
   166→    protocol_id: str,
   167→    pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]],
   168→) -> None:
   169→    """Batch-write audit log entries in a single session.
   170→
   171→    Args:
   172→        protocol_id: Protocol ID for context.
   173→        pending_audits: List of (criterion_id, entity, candidates, result) tuples.
   174→    """
   175→    if not pending_audits:
   176→        return
   177→    try:
   178→        with Session(engine) as session:
   179→            for criterion_id, entity, candidates, result in pending_audits:
   180→                _log_grounding_audit(
   181→                    session,
   182→                    protocol_id,
   183→                    criterion_id,
   184→                    entity,
   185→                    candidates,
   186→                    result,
   187→                )
   188→            session.commit()
   189→    except Exception as audit_error:
   190→        logger.warning(
   191→            "Failed to batch-write %d AuditLog entries: %s",
   192→            len(pending_audits),
   193→            audit_error,
   194→        )
   195→
   196→
   197→async def _ground_entity_with_retry(
   198→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   199→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   200→    criterion_text: str,
   201→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult, int]:
   202→    """Ground a single entity with agentic retry loop (max 3 attempts).
   203→
   204→    Implements the MedGemma agentic reasoning loop:
   205→    1. Initial grounding via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma
   206→    2. If low confidence, ask MedGemma 3 questions (valid? derived? rephrase?)
   207→    3. Retry with reformulated query (up to 2 more attempts, 3 total)
   208→    4. After 3 failed attempts, mark for expert_review
   209→
   210→    Args:
   211→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   212→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   213→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   214→
   215→    Returns:
   216→        Tuple of (EntityGroundingResult, attempt_count). May have expert_review
   217→        marker in reasoning.
   218→    """
   219→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   220→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   221→
   222→    # Step 1: Route entity through TerminologyRouter to get candidates
   223→    candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_text, entity_type)
   224→
   225→    # Log when TerminologyRouter returns empty
   226→    if not candidates:
   227→        apis = router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   228→        if not apis:
   229→            logger.info(
   230→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) skipped by TerminologyRouter"
   231→                " — no APIs configured for this type",
   232→                entity_text[:50],
   233→                entity_type,
   234→            )
   235→        else:
   236→            logger.info(
   237→                "Entity '%s' (type=%s) returned zero candidates from all APIs",
   238→                entity_text[:50],
   239→                entity_type,
   240→            )
   241→
   242→    # Step 2: MedGemma selects best match from candidates
   243→    result = await medgemma_decide(entity, candidates, criterion_text)
   244→
   245→    # Step 3: Agentic retry loop — max 3 attempts total
   246→    attempt = 1
   247→    current_query = entity_text
   248→    while (
   249→        result.selected_code is None
   250→        and result.confidence < 0.5
   251→        and attempt < 3
   252→        and router.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)
   253→    ):
   254→        attempt += 1
   255→        reasoning_result = await agentic_reasoning_loop(
   256→            entity={**entity, "_previous_query": current_query},
   257→            criterion_context=criterion_text,
   258→            router=router,
   259→            attempt=attempt,
   260→        )
   261→
   262→        if reasoning_result.should_skip:
   263→            logger.info(
   264→                "Entity '%s' marked non-medical by agentic reasoning"
   265→                " (attempt %d) — skipping",
   266→                entity_text[:50],
   267→                attempt,
   268→            )
   269→            return (
   270→                EntityGroundingResult(
   271→                    entity_text=entity_text,
   272→                    entity_type=entity_type,
   273→                    selected_code=None,
   274→                    selected_system=None,
   275→                    preferred_term=None,
   276→                    confidence=0.0,
   277→                    candidates=[],
   278→                    reasoning=(
   279→                        f"Skipped by agentic reasoning (attempt {attempt}): "
   280→                        f"{reasoning_result.reasoning}"
   281→                    ),
   282→                ),
   283→                attempt,
   284→            )
   285→
   286→        new_query = (
   287→            reasoning_result.rephrased_query
   288→            or reasoning_result.derived_term
   289→            or reasoning_result.gemini_suggestion
   290→            or current_query
   291→        )
   292→        current_query = new_query
   293→
   294→        logger.info(
   295→            "Agentic retry (attempt %d) for '%s' using query '%s'",
   296→            attempt,
   297→            entity_text[:50],
   298→            new_query[:50],
   299→        )
   300→
   301→        new_candidates = await router.route_entity(new_query, entity_type)
   302→        if new_candidates:
   303→            retry_entity = {**entity, "text": new_query}
   304→            result = await medgemma_decide(retry_entity, new_candidates, criterion_text)
   305→            if result.selected_code and result.confidence >= 0.5:
   306→                # Success — preserve original entity_text
   307→                return (
   308→                    EntityGroundingResult(
   309→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   310→                        entity_type=result.entity_type,
   311→                        selected_code=result.selected_code,
   312→                        selected_system=result.selected_system,
   313→                        preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   314→                        confidence=result.confidence,
   315→                        candidates=result.candidates,
   316→                        reasoning=(
   317→                            f"[Attempt {attempt}, query='{new_query}'] "
   318→                            f"{result.reasoning}"
   319→                        ),
   320→                        field_mappings=result.field_mappings,
   321→                    ),
   322→                    attempt,
   323→                )
   324→
   325→    # Route to expert_review if all attempts exhausted without success
   326→    if result.selected_code is None and attempt >= 2:
   327→        logger.warning(
   328→            "Entity '%s' (type=%s) routed to expert_review after"
   329→            " %d failed grounding attempts",
   330→            entity_text[:50],
   331→            entity_type,
   332→            attempt,
   333→        )
   334→        return (
   335→            EntityGroundingResult(
   336→                entity_text=result.entity_text,
   337→                entity_type=result.entity_type,
   338→                selected_code=result.selected_code,
   339→                selected_system=result.selected_system,
   340→                preferred_term=result.preferred_term,
   341→                confidence=result.confidence,
   342→                candidates=result.candidates,
   343→                reasoning=(
   344→                    f"[expert_review] Routed to expert review after "
   345→                    f"{attempt} failed grounding attempts. "
   346→                    f"Last reasoning: {result.reasoning}"
   347→                ),
   348→            ),
   349→            attempt,
   350→        )
   351→
   352→    return (result, attempt)
   353→
   354→
   355→async def _ground_entity_parallel(
   356→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   357→    router: TerminologyRouter,
   358→    criterion_text: str,
   359→    entity_num: int,
   360→    total: int,
   361→    semaphore: asyncio.Semaphore,
   362→) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult | None, str | None, float, int]:
   363→    """Ground a single entity with concurrency control and timing.
   364→
   365→    Acquires the semaphore before grounding to cap concurrent API calls.
   366→    Returns a (result, error) tuple for error isolation -- one entity failure
   367→    does not crash others in the asyncio.gather batch.
   368→
   369→    Args:
   370→        entity: Entity dict with text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   371→        router: TerminologyRouter singleton for API dispatch.
   372→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   373→        entity_num: 1-based index for logging.
   374→        total: Total entity count for logging.
   375→        semaphore: Concurrency limiter (Semaphore(4)).
   376→
   377→    Returns:
   378→        (EntityGroundingResult, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts) on success,
   379→        or (None, error_message, elapsed_ms, 0) on failure.
   380→    """
   381→    async with semaphore:
   382→        entity_text = entity.get("text", "")[:50]
   383→        entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   384→        start = time.monotonic()
   385→        logger.info(
   386→            "Grounding entity %d/%d: '%s' (type=%s) — start",
   387→            entity_num,
   388→            total,
   389→            entity_text,
   390→            entity_type,
   391→        )
   392→        try:
   393→            # Dual grounding: run TerminologyRouter + OMOP mapper
   394→            # in parallel per entity (Phase 1a)
   395→            full_entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   396→            tu_task = _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text)
   397→            omop_task = lookup_omop_concept(full_entity_text, entity_type)
   398→            (result, retry_attempts), omop_result = await asyncio.gather(
   399→                tu_task, omop_task
   400→            )
   401→
   402→            # Reconcile dual grounding results
   403→            result = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   404→
   405→            # Thread criterion_id from parse into result
   406→            criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id")
   407→            if criterion_id:
   408→                result.criterion_id = criterion_id
   409→
   410→            # Generate field mappings (same semaphore slot)
   411→            field_mappings = await generate_field_mappings(result, criterion_text)
   412→            result.field_mappings = field_mappings if field_mappings else None
   413→
   414→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   415→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   416→            logger.info(
   417→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' grounded in %.1fs: code=%s, omop=%s, conf=%.2f",
   418→                entity_num,
   419→                total,
   420→                entity_text,
   421→                elapsed,
   422→                result.selected_code or "N/A",
   423→                result.omop_concept_id or "N/A",
   424→                result.confidence,
   425→            )
   426→            return (result, None, elapsed_ms, retry_attempts)
   427→        except Exception as e:
   428→            elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   429→            elapsed_ms = elapsed * 1000
   430→            logger.error(
   431→                "Entity %d/%d '%s' failed in %.1fs: %s",
   432→                entity_num,
   433→                total,
   434→                entity_text,
   435→                elapsed,
   436→                e,
   437→            )
   438→            return (None, str(e), elapsed_ms, 0)
   439→
   440→
   441→async def ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict[str, Any]:
   442→    """Ground entities via TerminologyRouter + MedGemma.
   443→
   444→    Parses entities_json from state, routes each entity through TerminologyRouter
   445→    to get terminology candidates, then passes candidates to medgemma_decide for
   446→    best-match selection. Generates field mappings for each successfully grounded
   447→    entity.
   448→
   449→    Error accumulation: individual entity failures are logged and collected in
   450→    errors list. Processing continues with remaining entities. Only a total
   451→    failure to parse entities_json triggers a fatal error.
   452→
   453→    Args:
   454→        state: Current pipeline state with entities_json and protocol_id.
   455→
   456→    Returns:
   457→        Dict with grounded_entities_json and accumulated errors list,
   458→        or fatal error dict if entities_json cannot be parsed.
   459→    """
   460→    from protocol_processor.tracing import pipeline_span
   461→
   462→    if state.get("error"):
   463→        return {}
   464→
   465→    protocol_id = state.get("protocol_id", "")
   466→    with pipeline_span(
   467→        "ground_node", span_type="TOOL", protocol_id=protocol_id
   468→    ) as span:
   469→        span.set_inputs({"protocol_id": state.get("protocol_id", "")})
   470→
   471→        try:
   472→            entities_json = state.get("entities_json")
   473→            if not entities_json:
   474→                span.set_outputs({"error": "No entities_json in state"})
   475→                return {
   476→                    "error": "No entities_json in state — parse node may have failed",
   477→                }
   478→
   479→            entity_items: list[dict[str, Any]] = json.loads(entities_json)
   480→            protocol_id = state["protocol_id"]
   481→
   482→            span.set_inputs(
   483→                {
   484→                    "protocol_id": protocol_id,
   485→                    "entity_count": len(entity_items),
   486→                }
   487→            )
   488→
   489→            logger.info(
   490→                "Starting grounding for protocol %s: %d entities",
   491→                protocol_id,
   492→                len(entity_items),
   493→            )
   494→
   495→            # Fail fast: verify OMOP vocabulary is reachable before
   496→            # starting entity grounding. No silent fallback.
   497→            _get_omop_engine()
   498→
   499→            router = _get_router()
   500→            accumulated_errors: list[str] = list(state.get("errors") or [])
   501→            grounding_results: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   502→
   503→            # Pre-process: handle consent entities synchronously (skip grounding)
   504→            entities_to_ground: list[tuple[int, dict[str, Any]]] = []
   505→            for idx, entity in enumerate(entity_items, 1):
   506→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   507→                entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   508→
   509→                if entity_type == "Consent":
   510→                    logger.info(
   511→                        "Entity '%s' (type=Consent) skipped — consent/participation"
   512→                        " criteria are not groundable to terminology codes",
   513→                        entity_text[:50],
   514→                    )
   515→                    consent_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   516→                        entity_text=entity_text,
   517→                        entity_type=entity_type,
   518→                        selected_code=None,
   519→                        selected_system=None,
   520→                        preferred_term=None,
   521→                        confidence=0.0,
   522→                        candidates=[],
   523→                        reasoning=(
   524→                            "Consent/participation criterion — not groundable"
   525→                            " to medical terminology codes"
   526→                        ),
   527→                        criterion_id=entity.get("criterion_id"),
   528→                    )
   529→                    grounding_results.append(consent_result.model_dump())
   530→                else:
   531→                    entities_to_ground.append((idx, entity))
   532→
   533→            # Dev/debug: truncate entity list for faster runs and fewer API calls.
   534→            # DEV_MAX_ENTITIES takes precedence over PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES when set.
   535→            _max_entities = int(
   536→                os.getenv("DEV_MAX_ENTITIES")
   537→                or os.getenv("PIPELINE_MAX_ENTITIES")
   538→                or "0"
   539→            )
   540→            if _max_entities > 0:
   541→                entities_to_ground = entities_to_ground[:_max_entities]
   542→                logger.info(
   543→                    "Max entities limit=%d: truncated to %d entities",
   544→                    _max_entities,
   545→                    len(entities_to_ground),
   546→                )
   547→
   548→            # Pre-flight warmup: absorb MedGemma cold-start latency before
   549→            # the parallel batch. Non-fatal — if warmup fails, entities proceed.
   550→            try:
   551→                from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage as HumanMessageType
   552→
   553→                from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import (
   554→                    _get_medgemma_model,
   555→                )
   556→
   557→                warmup_model = _get_medgemma_model()
   558→                await warmup_model.ainvoke([HumanMessageType(content="ready")])
   559→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup succeeded")
   560→            except Exception as warmup_err:
   561→                logger.info("MedGemma warmup skipped: %s", warmup_err)
   562→
   563→            # Parallel grounding with asyncio.gather + semaphore
   564→            # Semaphore created here (not module-level) to bind to current event loop
   565→            semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
   566→
   567→            tasks = [
   568→                _ground_entity_parallel(
   569→                    entity,
   570→                    router,
   571→                    entity.get("criterion_text") or entity.get("text", ""),
   572→                    idx,
   573→                    len(entity_items),
   574→                    semaphore,
   575→                )
   576→                for idx, entity in entities_to_ground
   577→            ]
   578→
   579→            outcomes = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)
   580→
   581→            # Process outcomes and collect audit entries for batch write
   582→            pending_audits: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any], list[Any], Any]] = []
   583→            entity_times: list[float] = []
   584→            total_retry_count = 0
   585→            for (idx, entity), (result, error, elapsed_ms, retries) in zip(
   586→                entities_to_ground, outcomes
   587→            ):
   588→                entity_times.append(elapsed_ms)
   589→                total_retry_count += max(0, retries - 1)  # retries beyond first attempt
   590→                entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   591→                criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   592→
   593→                if result is not None:
   594→                    pending_audits.append(
   595→                        (criterion_id, entity, result.candidates, result)
   596→                    )
   597→                    grounding_results.append(result.model_dump())
   598→                else:
   599→                    # Entity failed — create expert_review fallback
   600→                    cid_short = criterion_id[:12] if criterion_id else "unknown"
   601→                    error_msg = (
   602→                        f"Entity grounding failed for '{entity_text[:50]}'"
   603→                        f" (criterion={cid_short}): {error}"
   604→                    )
   605→                    logger.error(error_msg)
   606→                    accumulated_errors.append(error_msg)
   607→
   608→            # Batch-write all audit log entries in a single session
   609→            _flush_audit_logs(protocol_id, pending_audits)
   610→
   611→            logger.info(
   612→                "Ground node complete for protocol %s: %d grounded, %d errors",
   613→                protocol_id,
   614→                len(grounding_results),
   615→                len(accumulated_errors),
   616→            )
   617→
   618→            span.set_outputs(
   619→                {
   620→                    "grounded_count": len(grounding_results),
   621→                    "error_count": len(accumulated_errors),
   622→                    "avg_entity_ms": (
   623→                        round(sum(entity_times) / len(entity_times))
   624→                        if entity_times
   625→                        else 0
   626→                    ),
   627→                    "max_entity_ms": round(max(entity_times)) if entity_times else 0,
   628→                    "retry_count": total_retry_count,
   629→                }
   630→            )
   631→
   632→            return {
   633→                "grounded_entities_json": json.dumps(grounding_results),
   634→                "errors": accumulated_errors,
   635→            }
   636→
   637→        except Exception as e:
   638→            logger.exception(
   639→                "Ground node fatal error for protocol %s: %s",
   640→                state.get("protocol_id", "unknown"),
   641→                e,
   642→            )
   643→            span.set_outputs({"error": str(e)})
   644→            return {"error": f"Ground node failed: {e}"}
   645→
```

     1→You are a clinical NLP entity extractor. Given a clinical trial eligibility criterion, extract ONLY the discrete medical concepts that should be grounded to terminology codes.
     2→
     3→## Rules
     4→1. Extract the medical ENTITY (not the full sentence). Examples:
     5→   - "eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2" -> text: "eGFR", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
     6→   - "No history of myocardial infarction" -> text: "myocardial infarction", entity_type: "Condition"
     7→   - "Currently taking calcium channel blockers" -> text: "calcium channel blockers", entity_type: "Medication"
     8→   - "Age 20 to 60 years" -> text: "age", entity_type: "Demographic"
     9→   - "BMI > 30 kg/m2" -> text: "body mass index", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
    10→   - "Coronary artery bypass graft within 3 months" -> text: "coronary artery bypass graft", entity_type: "Procedure"
    11→   - "Potassium >= 5.0 mmol/L" -> text: "potassium", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
    12→   - "LVEF <= 40%" -> text: "left ventricular ejection fraction", entity_type: "Lab_Value"
    13→
    14→2. One criterion may yield 0, 1, or multiple entities.
    15→3. Skip non-groundable concepts: consent, willingness, investigator discretion, geographic location, legal capacity.
    16→4. Entity types: Condition | Medication | Lab_Value | Procedure | Demographic | Other
    17→
    18→## Criterion
    19→{% if category %}Category hint: {{ category }}{% endif %}
    20→Text: "{{ criterion_text }}"
    21→
    22→Return a JSON object with key "entities" containing an array of {"text": string, "entity_type": string} objects.
    23→If no groundable entities exist, return {"entities": []}.
    24→

     1→You are a medical terminology expert. Your task is to select the best terminology code for a medical entity extracted from a clinical trial eligibility criterion.
     2→
     3→You will be given:
     4→- The entity text and type
     5→- The criterion context it came from
     6→- A list of terminology candidates from medical APIs (UMLS, SNOMED, RxNorm, etc.)
     7→
     8→Your job: Select the single best match or determine no match exists.
     9→
    10→## Confidence Scoring Guidelines
    11→
    12→- **0.9-1.0**: Exact match — entity text maps directly to the concept's preferred term or is a recognized synonym
    13→- **0.7-0.8**: Close synonym — entity is a common medical synonym (e.g., "heart attack" → "myocardial infarction")
    14→- **0.5-0.6**: Partial/broader match — concept is related but broader or narrower than the entity
    15→- **0.0**: No valid match or entity is not a meaningful medical concept
    16→
    17→## Rules
    18→
    19→1. Prefer exact or near-exact concept matches over broad categories
    20→2. For medications, prefer RxNorm over UMLS when both are available
    21→3. For conditions and procedures, UMLS or SNOMED is appropriate
    22→4. If the entity is a number, unit, or procedural language (not a medical concept), set confidence=0.0
    23→5. Always provide reasoning for your selection
    24→6. Output your analysis in plain text — the output will be structured by a downstream system
    25→
```

     2→Answer all three questions below to determine the best retry strategy.
     3→
     4→## Entity
     5→Text: "{{ entity_text }}"
     6→Type: {{ entity_type }}
     7→From criterion: "{{ criterion_context[:300] }}"
     8→{% if previous_query and previous_query != entity_text %}
     9→Previous search query: "{{ previous_query }}"
    10→{% endif %}
    11→Attempt: {{ attempt }} of 3
    12→
    13→## Three Reasoning Questions
    14→
    15→**Q1: Valid medical criterion?**
    16→Is "{{ entity_text }}" actually a valid medical criterion that should be grounded to a
    17→terminology code? Examples of entities that are NOT groundable: consent, participation,
    18→willingness, study enrollment, investigator discretion, geographic location.
    19→If it is not a valid medical criterion, return should_skip=true.
    20→
    21→**Q2: Derived entity?**
    22→Is "{{ entity_text }}" a derived entity that maps to a more standard medical concept?
    23→Examples:
    24→- "age >= 18 years" → the concept is "age" (the numeric value is a filter, not the concept)
    25→- "male" or "female" → maps to SNOMED gender concepts
    26→- "BMI > 30" → the concept is "body mass index"
    27→If derived, return is_derived=true and the standard medical concept term.
    28→
    29→**Q3: Rephrase for better terminology search?**
    30→Can "{{ entity_text }}" be rephrased as proper medical terminology to improve search results?
    31→Examples:
    32→- "high blood pressure" → "hypertension"
    33→- "sugar diabetes" → "diabetes mellitus"
    34→- "heart attack" → "myocardial infarction"
    35→- "kidney failure" → "renal failure"
    36→If rephrasing would help, return the rephrased query. Otherwise return null.
    37→
    38→## Instructions
    39→
    40→Respond with a brief clinical analysis. Address all three questions.
    41→State whether the entity should be skipped, whether it is a derived entity (and the
    42→standard term), and whether a rephrased query would improve terminology lookup.
    43→Be concise — one sentence per question.
    44→
