     1→"""Tests for OMOP mapper tool and dual grounding reconciliation logic.
     2→
     3→Tests cover:
     4→- _score_candidates: fuzzy scoring with exact/substring bonuses
     5→- _get_domain_filter: entity type to OMOP domain mapping
     6→- lookup_omop_concept: async entry point with mocked DB layer
     7→- _get_omop_engine: engine creation and validation
     8→- _reconcile_dual_grounding: TerminologyRouter + OMOP reconciliation
     9→
    10→All tests run without a live database. DB calls are mocked/patched.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import os
    16→from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch
    17→
    18→import pytest
    19→
    20→import protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper as omop_mapper_mod
    21→from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import _reconcile_dual_grounding
    22→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    23→
    24→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    25→# Helpers
    26→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    27→
    28→
    29→def _make_candidate(match_text: str, **extra: object) -> dict:
    30→    """Build a minimal candidate dict for _score_candidates."""
    31→    base = {
    32→        "concept_id": "12345",
    33→        "concept_name": match_text,
    34→        "domain_id": "Condition",
    35→        "vocabulary_id": "SNOMED",
    36→        "match_text": match_text,
    37→        "match_method": "concept_name",
    38→    }
    39→    base.update(extra)
    40→    return base
    41→
    42→
    43→def _make_grounding_result(**overrides: object) -> EntityGroundingResult:
    44→    """Build an EntityGroundingResult with sensible defaults."""
    45→    defaults: dict = {
    46→        "entity_text": "type 2 diabetes",
    47→        "entity_type": "Condition",
    48→        "selected_code": None,
    49→        "selected_system": None,
    50→        "preferred_term": None,
    51→        "confidence": 0.0,
    52→        "candidates": [],
    53→        "reasoning": "",
    54→    }
    55→    defaults.update(overrides)
    56→    return EntityGroundingResult(**defaults)
    57→
    58→
    59→# ===========================================================================
    60→# TestScoreCandidates
    61→# ===========================================================================
    62→
    63→
    64→class TestScoreCandidates:
    65→    """Tests for _score_candidates fuzzy scoring logic."""
    66→
    67→    def test_exact_match_gets_highest_score(self) -> None:
    68→        """An exact case-insensitive match should receive a 0.25 bonus."""
    69→        candidates = [_make_candidate("Type 2 Diabetes Mellitus")]
    70→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates(
    71→            "type 2 diabetes mellitus", candidates
    72→        )
    73→        # SequenceMatcher ratio for identical strings is 1.0
    74→        # bonus for exact match is 0.25, but capped at 1.0
    75→        assert scored[0]["score"] == 1.0
    76→
    77→    def test_substring_containment_gets_bonus(self) -> None:
    78→        """Substring containment (either direction) should add a 0.15 bonus."""
    79→        candidates = [_make_candidate("Type 2 Diabetes Mellitus")]
    80→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates("diabetes", candidates)
    81→        # "diabetes" is a substring of "type 2 diabetes mellitus"
    82→        # So bonus should be 0.15 (not 0.25, since not exact)
    83→        assert scored[0]["score"] > 0.0
    84→        # Verify by computing what the score would be without bonus
    85→        from difflib import SequenceMatcher
    86→
    87→        base = SequenceMatcher(None, "diabetes", "type 2 diabetes mellitus").ratio()
    88→        assert scored[0]["score"] == pytest.approx(base + 0.15, abs=1e-6)
    89→
    90→    def test_non_matching_gets_only_base_score(self) -> None:
    91→        """A candidate with no substring overlap gets only the base score."""
    92→        candidates = [_make_candidate("Hypertension")]
    93→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates("metformin", candidates)
    94→        from difflib import SequenceMatcher
    95→
    96→        expected_base = SequenceMatcher(None, "metformin", "hypertension").ratio()
    97→        assert scored[0]["score"] == pytest.approx(expected_base, abs=1e-6)
    98→
    99→    def test_candidates_sorted_descending(self) -> None:
   100→        """Candidates must be sorted by score in descending order."""
   101→        candidates = [
   102→            _make_candidate("Hypertension"),
   103→            _make_candidate("Type 2 Diabetes Mellitus"),
   104→            _make_candidate("Diabetes"),
   105→        ]
   106→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates("diabetes", candidates)
   107→        scores = [c["score"] for c in scored]
   108→        assert scores == sorted(scores, reverse=True)
   109→
   110→    def test_empty_candidates_returns_empty(self) -> None:
   111→        """An empty candidate list should return an empty list."""
   112→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates("diabetes", [])
   113→        assert scored == []
   114→
   115→    def test_score_capped_at_one(self) -> None:
   116→        """Score must never exceed 1.0 even with bonus."""
   117→        # Exact match: base=1.0 + bonus=0.25 should be capped to 1.0
   118→        candidates = [_make_candidate("metformin")]
   119→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates("metformin", candidates)
   120→        assert scored[0]["score"] <= 1.0
   121→
   122→    def test_case_insensitive_matching(self) -> None:
   123→        """Scoring should be case-insensitive."""
   124→        candidates = [_make_candidate("METFORMIN")]
   125→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates("metformin", candidates)
   126→        # Exact case-insensitive match
   127→        assert scored[0]["score"] == 1.0
   128→
   129→    def test_whitespace_stripped(self) -> None:
   130→        """Leading/trailing whitespace should not affect scoring."""
   131→        candidates = [_make_candidate("  metformin  ")]
   132→        scored = omop_mapper_mod._score_candidates("  metformin  ", candidates)
   133→        assert scored[0]["score"] == 1.0
   134→

   293→class TestReconcileDualGrounding:
   294→    """Tests for _reconcile_dual_grounding from ground.py."""
   295→
   296→    def test_both_succeed_matching_terms_agree(self) -> None:
   297→        """Both TU and OMOP succeed with overlapping terms: 'agree'."""
   298→        result = _make_grounding_result(
   299→            selected_code="C0011847",
   300→            preferred_term="Type 2 Diabetes Mellitus",
   301→        )
   302→        omop_result = omop_mapper_mod.OmopLookupResult(
   303→            omop_concept_id="201826",
   304→            omop_concept_name="Type 2 Diabetes Mellitus",
   305→            match_score=0.95,
   306→        )
   307→
   308→        reconciled = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   309→
   310→        assert reconciled.reconciliation_status == "agree"
   311→        assert reconciled.omop_concept_id == "201826"
   312→
   313→    def test_both_succeed_substring_match_agree(self) -> None:
   314→        """Substring containment counts as agreement."""
   315→        result = _make_grounding_result(
   316→            selected_code="C0011847",
   317→            preferred_term="Diabetes",
   318→        )
   319→        omop_result = omop_mapper_mod.OmopLookupResult(
   320→            omop_concept_id="201826",
   321→            omop_concept_name="Type 2 Diabetes Mellitus",
   322→            match_score=0.90,
   323→        )
   324→
   325→        reconciled = _reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)
   326→
   327→        assert reconciled.reconciliation_status == "agree"

   329→    def test_both_succeed_non_matching_terms_disagreement(self) -> None:
   330→        """Both succeed but terms don't overlap: 'disagreement'."""
   331→        result = _make_grounding_result(
   332→            selected_code="C0011847",

     1→"""Unit and value normalizer: UCUM lookup with OMOP concept ID resolution.
     2→
     3→Phase 3 (Gap 7): Deterministic YAML-based lookup mapping common clinical
     4→trial units to UCUM codes and OMOP unit_concept_id values, plus optional
     5→categorical value normalization (e.g. "positive" -> SNOMED qualifier concept).
     6→
     7→Phase 3b: Ordinal scale normalization (ECOG, Karnofsky, NYHA, etc.) with
     8→agent-assisted concept resolution.  Recognized ordinal entities get
     9→unit_concept_id=8527 ({score}); value_concept_id comes from YAML config
    10→(populated incrementally via agent/approval workflow).
    11→
    12→No LLM calls — pure static lookup with alias expansion and case-insensitive
    13→matching.  Returns (None, None) for unrecognized inputs; never raises.
    14→"""
    15→
    16→from __future__ import annotations
    17→
    18→import logging
    19→import re
    20→from functools import lru_cache
    21→from pathlib import Path
    22→from typing import Any
    23→
    24→import yaml
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / "config" / "ucum_mappings.yaml"
    29→
    30→_ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE = re.compile(
    31→    r"^(?:grade|stage|class|score|level)\s*",
    32→    re.IGNORECASE,
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
    37→def _load_ucum_mappings() -> tuple[dict[str, tuple[str, int]], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Load and index the UCUM mappings YAML (cached after first call).
    39→
    40→    Returns:
    41→        Tuple of (unit_lookup, value_lookup) where:
    42→        - unit_lookup: lowercased alias/canonical -> (canonical, omop_id)
    43→        - value_lookup: lowercased value text -> omop_value_concept_id
    44→    """
    45→    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
    46→        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)
    47→
    48→    unit_lookup: dict[str, tuple[str, int]] = {}
    49→    for entry in data.get("units", []):
    50→        canonical: str = entry["canonical"]
    51→        omop_id: int = entry["omop_unit_concept_id"]
    52→        # Index canonical form
    53→        unit_lookup[canonical.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    54→        # Index all aliases
    55→        for alias in entry.get("aliases", []):
    56→            unit_lookup[alias.strip().lower()] = (canonical, omop_id)
    57→
    58→    value_lookup: dict[str, int] = {}
    59→    for text, concept_id in data.get("value_mappings", {}).items():
    60→        value_lookup[str(text).strip().lower()] = int(concept_id)
    61→
    62→    return unit_lookup, value_lookup
    63→
    64→
    65→def normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    66→    """Normalize a unit string to its UCUM code and OMOP unit_concept_id.
    67→
    68→    Args:
    69→        unit_text: Raw unit text (e.g. "mg/dL", "%", "years").
    70→
    71→    Returns:
    72→        Tuple of (ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id), or (None, None)
    73→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    74→    """
    75→    if unit_text is not None and not isinstance(unit_text, str):
    76→        unit_text = str(unit_text)
    77→    if not unit_text or not unit_text.strip():
    78→        return (None, None)
    79→
    80→    key = unit_text.strip().lower()
    81→    unit_lookup, _ = _load_ucum_mappings()
    82→    result = unit_lookup.get(key)
    83→    if result is not None:
    84→        return result
    85→    return (None, None)
    86→
    87→
    88→def normalize_value(
    89→    value_text: str | None,
    90→) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
    91→    """Normalize a categorical value to its SNOMED code and OMOP concept ID.
    92→
    93→    Args:
    94→        value_text: Raw value text (e.g. "positive", "negative", "normal").
    95→
    96→    Returns:
    97→        Tuple of (normalized_text, omop_value_concept_id), or (None, None)
    98→        if the input is None, empty, or unrecognized.
    99→    """
   100→    if value_text is not None and not isinstance(value_text, str):
   101→        value_text = str(value_text)
   102→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
   103→        return (None, None)
   104→
   105→    key = value_text.strip().lower()
   106→    _, value_lookup = _load_ucum_mappings()
   107→    concept_id = value_lookup.get(key)
   108→    if concept_id is not None:
   109→        return (key, concept_id)
   110→    return (None, None)
   111→
   112→
   113→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   114→# Ordinal scale normalization (Phase 3b)
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→
   117→
   118→@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
   119→def _load_ordinal_scales() -> tuple[dict[str, str], dict[str, Any]]:
   120→    """Load ordinal scale definitions from YAML config (cached).
   121→
   122→    Returns:
   123→        Tuple of (alias_to_scale, scale_defs) where:
   124→        - alias_to_scale: lowercased alias -> scale key (e.g. "ecog")
   125→        - scale_defs: raw ordinal_scales dict from YAML
   126→    """
   127→    with open(_CONFIG_PATH) as f:
   128→        data: dict[str, Any] = yaml.safe_load(f)
   129→
   130→    scale_defs: dict[str, Any] = data.get("ordinal_scales", {})
   131→    alias_to_scale: dict[str, str] = {}
   132→    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():
   133→        for alias in scale_def.get("entity_aliases", []):
   134→            alias_to_scale[alias.strip().lower()] = scale_key
   135→
   136→    return alias_to_scale, scale_defs
   137→
   138→
   139→def _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text: str) -> str | None:
   140→    """Match entity text to an ordinal scale key.
   141→
   142→    Uses three strategies in order:
   143→    1. Exact alias match (case-insensitive)
   144→    2. Entity text is a substring of an alias
   145→    3. An alias is a substring of entity text
   146→
   147→    Returns:
   148→        Scale key (e.g. "ecog") or None if no match.
   149→    """
   150→    alias_to_scale, _ = _load_ordinal_scales()
   151→    key = entity_text.strip().lower()
   152→
   153→    # Strategy 1: exact alias match
   154→    if key in alias_to_scale:
   155→        return alias_to_scale[key]
   156→
   157→    # Strategy 2 & 3: bidirectional substring containment
   158→    for alias, scale_key in alias_to_scale.items():
   159→        if key in alias or alias in key:
   160→            return scale_key
   161→
   162→    return None
   163→
   164→
   165→def normalize_ordinal_value(
   166→    value_text: str | None,
   167→    entity_text: str | None = None,
   168→) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None:
   169→    """Entity-context-aware ordinal value normalization.
   170→
   171→    Returns:
   172→        (value_concept_id, unit_concept_id) if entity matches an ordinal scale.
   173→        value_concept_id is the approved OMOP ID from YAML (or None if not yet
   174→        resolved).  unit_concept_id is always 8527 ({score}) for ordinal scales.
   175→        Returns None (not a tuple) if entity doesn't match any ordinal scale.
   176→    """
   177→    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():
   178→        return None
   179→
   180→    scale_key = _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text)
   181→    if scale_key is None:
   182→        return None
   183→
   184→    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()
   185→    scale_def = scale_defs[scale_key]
   186→    unit_concept_id: int | None = scale_def.get("unit_concept_id")
   187→
   188→    if not value_text or not value_text.strip():
   189→        return (None, unit_concept_id)
   190→
   191→    # Normalize value: strip ordinal prefixes, convert "2.0" -> "2"
   192→    cleaned = _ORDINAL_PREFIX_RE.sub("", value_text.strip())
   193→    try:
   194→        numeric = float(cleaned)
   195→        if numeric == int(numeric):
   196→            cleaned = str(int(numeric))
   197→        else:
   198→            cleaned = str(numeric)
   199→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
   200→        cleaned = cleaned.strip()
   201→
   202→    values = scale_def.get("values", {})
   203→    val_entry = values.get(cleaned)
   204→    if val_entry is None:
   205→        # Grade not in the defined values for this scale
   206→        return (None, unit_concept_id)
   207→
   208→    omop_value_cid = val_entry.get("omop_value_concept_id")
   209→    return (omop_value_cid, unit_concept_id)
   210→
   211→
   212→def propose_ordinal_mappings() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   213→    """Scan ordinal_scales config and return entries missing omop_value_concept_id.
   214→
   215→    Returns a list of dicts, each with:
   216→      - scale: scale key (e.g., "ecog")
   217→      - grade: value string (e.g., "2")
   218→      - snomed_code: known SNOMED code (if any)
   219→      - loinc_answer: known LOINC answer code (if any)
   220→      - description: human-readable description
   221→      - status: "needs_resolution"
   222→
   223→    This output can be fed to an LLM agent or OMOP vocabulary lookup to propose
   224→    omop_value_concept_id values.  Once approved, add them to the YAML.
   225→    """
   226→    _, scale_defs = _load_ordinal_scales()
   227→    missing: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
   228→
   229→    for scale_key, scale_def in scale_defs.items():
   230→        for grade, val_entry in scale_def.get("values", {}).items():
   231→            if val_entry.get("omop_value_concept_id") is not None:
   232→                continue
   233→            missing.append(
   234→                {
   235→                    "scale": scale_key,
   236→                    "grade": grade,
   237→                    "snomed_code": val_entry.get("snomed_code"),
   238→                    "loinc_answer": val_entry.get("loinc_answer"),
   239→                    "description": val_entry.get("description"),
   240→                    "status": "needs_resolution",
   241→                }
   242→            )
   243→
   244→    return missing
   245→
```

     1→"""Pydantic schemas for terminology grounding results and API candidates.
     2→
     3→These schemas define the machine-readable format for agent-to-agent communication
     4→between TerminologyRouter and MedGemma during the grounding phase.
     5→
     6→Per user decision: "Agent-to-agent communication must be machine-readable
     7→(no superfluous commentary between agents)."
     8→"""
     9→
    10→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    11→
    12→
    13→class GroundingCandidate(BaseModel):
    14→    """A single candidate match from a terminology API.
    15→
    16→    Attributes:
    17→        source_api: Name of the API that produced this candidate.
    18→        code: Terminology code (e.g. UMLS CUI "C0011847", RxNorm "6809").
    19→        preferred_term: Canonical preferred name for the concept.
    20→        semantic_type: UMLS semantic type. None for non-UMLS APIs.
    21→        score: Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.
    22→    """
    23→
    24→    source_api: str = Field(
    25→        description="Name of the terminology API that produced this candidate"
    26→    )
    27→    code: str = Field(description="Terminology code (CUI, RxNorm, ICD-10, etc.)")
    28→    preferred_term: str = Field(description="Canonical preferred name for the concept")
    29→    semantic_type: str | None = Field(
    30→        default=None,
    31→        description=(
    32→            "UMLS semantic type (e.g. 'Disease or Syndrome'). None for non-UMLS APIs."
    33→        ),
    34→    )
    35→    score: float = Field(
    36→        ge=0.0,
    37→        le=1.0,
    38→        description="Relevance score from the API (0.0-1.0). Higher is better.",
    39→    )
    40→
    41→
    42→class EntityGroundingResult(BaseModel):
    43→    """Grounding result for a single entity after MedGemma evaluation.
    44→
    45→    Contains the selected code, all candidates considered, and the reasoning
    46→    used to make the selection. Provides full audit trail per user decision:
    47→    "All agent exchanges persisted to database."
    48→
    49→    Attributes:
    50→        entity_text: Original entity text as extracted from the protocol.
    51→        entity_type: Entity type (e.g. "Medication", "Condition").
    52→        selected_code: The chosen terminology code. None if grounding failed.
    53→        selected_system: The coding system for the selected code.
    54→        preferred_term: Canonical name for the selected code.
    55→        confidence: MedGemma's confidence in the selection (0.0-1.0).
    56→        candidates: All candidates returned by terminology APIs.
    57→        reasoning: MedGemma's explanation for selecting this code.
    58→        field_mappings: Suggested AutoCriteria field mappings
    59→            (Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time). None if not yet generated.
    60→    """
    61→
    62→    entity_text: str = Field(description="Original entity text from the protocol")
    63→    entity_type: str = Field(description="Entity type (e.g. 'Medication', 'Condition')")
    64→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
    65→        default=None,
    66→        description="The chosen terminology code. None if grounding failed.",
    67→    )
    68→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
    69→        default=None,
    70→        description="The coding system for the selected code (e.g. 'UMLS').",
    71→    )
    72→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
    73→        default=None,
    74→        description="Canonical name for the selected code.",
    75→    )
    76→    confidence: float = Field(
    77→        ge=0.0,
    78→        le=1.0,
    79→        description="MedGemma's confidence in the selection (0.0-1.0).",
    80→    )
    81→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate] = Field(
    82→        default_factory=list,
    83→        description="All candidates returned by terminology APIs.",
    84→    )
    85→    reasoning: str = Field(
    86→        default="",
    87→        description="MedGemma's explanation for selecting this code.",
    88→    )
    89→    field_mappings: list[dict] | None = Field(
    90→        default=None,
    91→        description=(
    92→            "Suggested AutoCriteria field mappings "
    93→            "(Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time)."
    94→        ),
    95→    )
    96→    criterion_id: str | None = Field(
    97→        default=None,
    98→        description=(
    99→            "FK to Criteria record this entity belongs to (threaded from parse)."
   100→        ),
   101→    )
   102→    omop_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   103→        default=None,
   104→        description=(
   105→            "OMOP standard concept_id from Athena vocabulary lookup. "
   106→            "Used for CIRCE export and OMOP CDM joins."
   107→        ),
   108→    )
   109→    reconciliation_status: str | None = Field(
   110→        default=None,
   111→        description=(
   112→            "Dual grounding reconciliation: 'agree', 'disagreement', "
   113→            "'omop_missing', 'tooluniverse_missing'."
   114→        ),
   115→    )
   116→
   117→
   118→class GroundingBatchResult(BaseModel):
   119→    """Batch grounding result for all entities in a criteria batch.
   120→
   121→    Implements the error accumulation pattern — partial failures are preserved
   122→    alongside successes. Per user decision: "Errors accumulate — process all
   123→    entities, collect errors alongside successes (partial results are useful)."
   124→
   125→    Attributes:
   126→        results: Successfully grounded entity results.
   127→        errors: Error messages for entities that failed grounding.
   128→    """
   129→
   130→    results: list[EntityGroundingResult] = Field(
   131→        default_factory=list,
   132→        description="Successfully grounded entity results.",
   133→    )
   134→    errors: list[str] = Field(
   135→        default_factory=list,
   136→        description="Error messages for entities that failed grounding.",
   137→    )
   138→

Examples:\n   - \"eGFR >= 30 mL/min/1.73m2\" -> text: \"eGFR\", entity_type: \"Lab_Value\"\n   - \"No history of myocardial infarction\" -> text: \"myocardial infarction\", entity_type: \"Condition\"\n   - \"Currently taking calcium channel blockers\" -> text: \"calcium channel blockers\", entity_type: \"Medication\"\n   - \"Age 20 to 60 years\" -> text: \"age\", entity_type: \"Demographic\"\n   - \"BMI > 30 kg/m2\" -> text: \"body mass index\", entity_type: \"Lab_Value\"\n   - \"Coronary artery bypass graft within 3 months\" -> text: \"coronary artery bypass graft\", entity_type: \"Procedure\"\n   - \"Potassium >= 5.0 mmol/L\" -> text: \"potassium\", entity_type: \"Lab_Value\"\n   - \"LVEF <= 40%\" -> text: \"left ventricular ejection fraction\", entity_type: \"Lab_Value\"\n\n2. One criterion may yield 0, 1, or multiple entities.\n3. Skip non-groundable concepts: consent, willingness, investigator discretion, geographic location, legal capacity.\n4. Entity types: Condition | Medication | Lab_Value | Procedure | Demographic | Other\n5. The `value` field is NOT used by this tool. Only extract `text` (the medical concept) and `entity_type`.\n\n## Compound Concept Rules — DO NOT Over-Decompose\n- Do NOT separate anatomical modifiers, severities, laterality, or procedure types from their parent concept. These are a single medical entity:\n  - \"tricompartmental knee replacement\" → ONE entity (not \"knee replacement\" + \"tricompartmental\")\n  - \"spinal muscular atrophy\" → ONE entity\n  - \"non-small cell lung cancer\" → ONE entity\n  - \"left ventricular hypertrophy\" → ONE entity\n  - \"chronic obstructive pulmonary disease\" → ONE entity\n  - \"bilateral total hip arthroplasty\" → ONE entity\n- DO separate distinct comorbidities or co-occurring conditions into individual entities:\n  - \"Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy\" → TWO entities: \"type 2 diabetes\" AND \"nephropathy\"\n  - \"Hypertension and heart failure\" → TWO entities: \"hypertension\" AND \"heart failure\"\n  - \"Asthma or COPD\" → TWO entities: \"asthma\" AND \"chronic obstructive pulmonary disease\""
}

  "old_string": "class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.\n\n    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into\n    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    entity: str = Field(description=\"The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')\")\n    relation: str = Field(\n        description=\"The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')\"\n    )\n    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(\n        description=\"Typed value object with type discriminator\"\n    )\n    unit: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')\",\n    )",
  "new_string": "# The set of valid relation operators accepted by the frontend\nRelationOperator = Literal[\n    \"=\", \"!=\", \">\", \">=\", \"<\", \"<=\", \"within\", \"not_in_last\", \"contains\", \"not_contains\"\n]\n\n\nclass FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):\n    \"\"\"A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.\n\n    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into\n    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    entity: str = Field(description=\"The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')\")\n    relation: RelationOperator = Field(\n        description=\"The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')\"\n    )\n    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(\n        description=\"Typed value object with type discriminator\"\n    )\n    unit: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')\",\n    )\n    value_concept_id: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"OMOP concept ID for categorical values (e.g. 'Severe', 'Stage IV')\",\n    )\n    value_concept_system: str | None = Field(\n        default=None,\n        description=\"Terminology system for value_concept_id (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'OMOP')\",\n    )\n\n    @field_validator(\"relation\", mode=\"before\")\n    @classmethod\n    def normalize_relation(cls, v: str) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Normalize LLM-generated relation operators before Literal validation.\"\"\"\n        if isinstance(v, str):\n            return _normalize_relation(v)\n        return v"
}

  "new_string": "## Rules\n\n1. Prefer exact or near-exact concept matches over broad categories\n2. For medications, prefer RxNorm over UMLS when both are available\n3. For conditions and procedures, UMLS or SNOMED is appropriate\n4. If the entity is a number, unit, or procedural language (not a medical concept), set confidence=0.0\n5. Always provide reasoning for your selection\n6. Output your analysis in plain text — the output will be structured by a downstream system\n\n## Search Strategy (for retry attempts)\n\nIf your first terminology search yields 0 results:\n- DO NOT retry the exact same phrase or minor word-order variations.\n- Simplify to the core medical noun: \"Left ventricular hypertrophy\" → \"ventricular hypertrophy\" → \"hypertrophy\".\n- Drop anatomical qualifiers first, then adjectives: \"bilateral chronic kidney disease\" → \"chronic kidney disease\" → \"kidney disease\".\n- Expand abbreviations: \"HTN\" → \"hypertension\", \"MI\" → \"myocardial infarction\", \"DM\" → \"diabetes mellitus\", \"CKD\" → \"chronic kidney disease\", \"COPD\" → \"chronic obstructive pulmonary disease\".\n- Try the generic drug class if a specific brand name fails: \"Keytruda\" → \"pembrolizumab\" → \"anti-PD-1 monoclonal antibody\".\n- Each retry MUST use a substantially different query from all previous attempts."
}

Fallback domains follow.\nCandidates from the primary domain receive a small scoring bonus.\n\"\"\"\n\nPRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS: float = 0.05\n\"\"\"Small scoring bonus applied to candidates from the primary (first) domain.\"\"\""

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py",
  "old_string": "def _get_domain_filter(entity_type: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Resolve entity type to an OMOP domain_id filter value.\n\n    Falls back to ``\"Observation\"`` for unknown entity types.\n\n    Args:\n        entity_type: Pipeline entity type (e.g. \"Condition\", \"Medication\").\n\n    Returns:\n        OMOP domain_id string.\n    \"\"\"\n    domain = ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN.get(entity_type, \"Observation\")\n    if entity_type not in ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN:\n        logger.info(\n            \"Entity type '%s' not in OMOP domain map — defaulting to '%s'\",\n            entity_type,\n            domain,\n        )\n    return domain",
  "new_string": "def _get_domain_filter(entity_type: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Resolve entity type to an OMOP domain_id filter value.\n\n    Falls back to ``\"Observation\"`` for unknown entity types.\n\n    Args:\n        entity_type: Pipeline entity type (e.g. \"Condition\", \"Medication\").\n\n    Returns:\n        OMOP domain_id string.\n    \"\"\"\n    domain = ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN.get(entity_type, \"Observation\")\n    if entity_type not in ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAIN:\n        logger.info(\n            \"Entity type '%s' not in OMOP domain map — defaulting to '%s'\",\n            entity_type,\n            domain,\n        )\n    return domain\n\n\ndef _get_domain_filters(entity_type: str) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Resolve entity type to a list of OMOP domain_id filter values.\n\n    Returns primary + fallback domains. The first domain in the list\n    is the primary domain (candidates from it receive a scoring bonus).\n    Falls back to ``[\"Observation\"]`` for unknown entity types.\n\n    Args:\n        entity_type: Pipeline entity type (e.g. \"Condition\", \"Medication\").\n\n    Returns:\n        List of OMOP domain_id strings, primary first.\n    \"\"\"\n    domains = ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAINS.get(entity_type)\n    if domains is not None:\n        return domains\n    logger.info(\n        \"Entity type '%s' not in OMOP multi-domain map — defaulting to ['Observation']\",\n        entity_type,\n    )\n    return [\"Observation\"]"
}

{

  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py",
  "old_string": "def _query_concept_table(\n    engine: Any, entity_text: str, domain_id: str\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Query concept table for standard concepts matching *entity_text*.\n\n    Args:\n        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.\n        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).\n        domain_id: OMOP domain_id filter.\n\n    Returns:\n        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,\n        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.\n    \"\"\"\n    sql = text(\n        \"SELECT concept_id, concept_name, domain_id, vocabulary_id \"\n        \"FROM concept \"\n        \"WHERE standard_concept = 'S' \"\n        \"  AND domain_id = :domain_id \"\n        \"  AND concept_name ILIKE :pattern \"\n        \"ORDER BY length(concept_name) ASC \"\n        \"LIMIT :max_candidates\"\n    )\n    pattern = f\"%{entity_text}%\"\n    with engine.connect() as conn:\n        rows = conn.execute(\n            sql,\n            {\n                \"domain_id\": domain_id,\n                \"pattern\": pattern,\n                \"max_candidates\": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,\n            },\n        ).fetchall()\n\n    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for row in rows:\n        candidates.append(\n            {\n                \"concept_id\": str(row[0]),\n                \"concept_name\": row[1],\n                \"domain_id\": row[2],\n                \"vocabulary_id\": row[3],\n                \"match_text\": row[1],\n                \"match_method\": \"concept_name\",\n            }\n        )\n    return candidates",
  "new_string": "def _query_concept_table(\n    engine: Any, entity_text: str, domain_ids: list[str]\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Query concept table for standard concepts matching *entity_text*.\n\n    Args:\n        engine: SQLAlchemy engine for the OMOP vocabulary database.\n        entity_text: Text to search for (used in ILIKE pattern).\n        domain_ids: List of OMOP domain_id values to filter on.\n\n    Returns:\n        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,\n        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Build parameterized IN clause for domain_ids\n    domain_params = {f\"d{i}\": d for i, d in enumerate(domain_ids)}\n    domain_placeholders = \", \".join(f\":d{i}\" for i in range(len(domain_ids)))\n\n    sql = text(\n        \"SELECT concept_id, concept_name, domain_id, vocabulary_id \"\n        \"FROM concept \"\n        \"WHERE standard_concept = 'S' \"\n        f\"  AND domain_id IN ({domain_placeholders}) \"\n        \"  AND concept_name ILIKE :pattern \"\n        \"ORDER BY length(concept_name) ASC \"\n        \"LIMIT :max_candidates\"\n    )\n    pattern = f\"%{entity_text}%\"\n    params = {\n        **domain_params,\n        \"pattern\": pattern,\n        \"max_candidates\": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,\n    }\n    with engine.connect() as conn:\n        rows = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall()\n\n    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for row in rows:\n        candidates.append(\n            {\n                \"concept_id\": str(row[0]),\n                \"concept_name\": row[1],\n                \"domain_id\": row[2],\n                \"vocabulary_id\": row[3],\n                \"match_text\": row[1],\n                \"match_method\": \"concept_name\",\n            }\n        )\n    return candidates"
}

may be ambiguous).\n\n    Returns:\n        List of candidate dicts with keys: concept_id, concept_name,\n        domain_id, vocabulary_id, match_text, match_method.\n    \"\"\"\n    pattern = f\"%{entity_text}%\"\n\n    if skip_domain_filter:\n        sql = text(\n            \"SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name, c.domain_id, \"\n            \"       c.vocabulary_id, s.concept_synonym_name \"\n            \"FROM concept_synonym s \"\n            \"JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id \"\n            \"WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' \"\n            \"  AND s.concept_synonym_name ILIKE :pattern \"\n            \"ORDER BY length(s.concept_synonym_name) ASC \"\n            \"LIMIT :max_candidates\"\n        )\n        params: dict[str, Any] = {\n domain_placeholders = \", \".join(f\":d{i}\" for i in range(len(domain_ids)))\n        sql = text(\n            \"SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_name, c.domain_id, \"\n            \"       c.vocabulary_id, s.concept_synonym_name \"\n            \"FROM concept_synonym s \"\n            \"JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id \"\n            \"WHERE c.standard_concept = 'S' \"\n \"LIMIT :max_candidates\"\n        )\n        params = {\n            **domain_params,\n            \"pattern\": pattern,\n            \"max_candidates\": OMOP_MAX_CANDIDATES,\n        }\n\n    with engine.connect() as conn:\n        rows = conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall()\n\n    candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []\n    for row in rows:\n        candidates.append(\n            {\n                \"concept_id\": str(row[0]),\n                \"concept_name\": row[1],\n                \"domain_id\": row[2],\n                \"vocabulary_id\": row[3],\n                \"match_text\": row[4],  # synonym name used for scoring\n                \"match_method\": \"synonym\",\n            }\n        )\n    return candidates"

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py",
  "old_string": "def _sync_lookup(entity_text: str, domain_id: str) -> OmopLookupResult:\n    \"\"\"Synchronous OMOP lookup — runs inside run_in_executor.\n\n    Queries both concept and concept_synonym tables, deduplicates\n    candidates by concept_id (preferring concept_name match), scores them,\n    and returns the best match above the threshold.\n\n    Args:\n        entity_text: The entity text to look up.\n        domain_id: OMOP domain_id to filter on.\n\n    Returns:\n        OmopLookupResult with best match, or empty result if nothing found\n        or all candidates score below OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE.\n\n    Raises:\n        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not configured.\n        Exception: Database errors are propagated, not swallowed.\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = _get_omop_engine()\n\n    concept_candidates = _query_concept_table(engine, entity_text, domain_id)\n    synonym_candidates = _query_synonym_table(engine, entity_text, domain_id)\n\n    # Deduplicate by concept_id — prefer concept_name match over synonym\n    seen: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n    for candidate in concept_candidates:\n        seen[candidate[\"concept_id\"]] = candidate\n    for candidate in synonym_candidates:\n        if candidate[\"concept_id\"] not in seen:\n            seen[candidate[\"concept_id\"]] = candidate\n\n    all_candidates = list(seen.values())\n\n    if not all_candidates:\n        logger.debug(\n            \"No OMOP candidates found for '%s' in domain '%s'\",\n            entity_text[:50],\n            domain_id,\n        )\n        return OmopLookupResult()\n\n    # Score and sort\n    scored = _score_candidates(entity_text, all_candidates)\n    best = scored[0]\n\n    if best[\"score\"] < OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE:\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Best OMOP candidate for '%s' scored %.3f \"\n            \"(below threshold %.2f) — skipping\",\n            entity_text[:50],\n            best[\"score\"],\n            OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE,\n        )\n        return OmopLookupResult()\n\n    logger.info(\n        \"OMOP match for '%s': concept_id=%s, name='%s', score=%.3f, method=%s\",\n        entity_text[:50],\n        best[\"concept_id\"],\n        best[\"concept_name\"][:60],\n        best[\"score\"],\n        best[\"match_method\"],\n    )\n\n    return OmopLookupResult(\n        omop_concept_id=best[\"concept_id\"],\n        omop_concept_name=best[\"concept_name\"],\n        omop_vocabulary_id=best[\"vocabulary_id\"],\n        omop_domain_id=best[\"domain_id\"],\n        match_score=best[\"score\"],\n        match_method=best[\"match_method\"],\n    )",
  "new_string": "def _sync_lookup(\n    entity_text: str,\n    domain_ids: list[str],\n    *,\n    is_acronym: bool = False,\n) -> OmopLookupResult:\n    \"\"\"Synchronous OMOP lookup — runs inside run_in_executor.\n\n    Queries both concept and concept_synonym tables, deduplicates\n    candidates by concept_id (preferring concept_name match), scores them,\n    and returns the best match above the threshold.\n\n    Args:\n        entity_text: The entity text to look up.\n        domain_ids: List of OMOP domain_id values to filter on.\n            The first domain is considered \"primary\" and gets a scoring bonus.\n        is_acronym: If True, skip domain filter for synonym search\n            (acronyms may appear in any OMOP domain).\n\n    Returns:\n        OmopLookupResult with best match, or empty result if nothing found\n        or all candidates score below OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE.\n\n    Raises:\n        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not configured.\n        Exception: Database errors are propagated, not swallowed.\n    \"\"\"\n    engine = _get_omop_engine()\n\n    concept_candidates = _query_concept_table(engine, entity_text, domain_ids)\n    synonym_candidates = _query_synonym_table(\n        engine,\n        entity_text,\n        domain_ids,\n        skip_domain_filter=is_acronym,\n    )\n\n    # Deduplicate by concept_id — prefer concept_name match over synonym\n    seen: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}\n    for candidate in concept_candidates:\n        seen[candidate[\"concept_id\"]] = candidate\n    for candidate in synonym_candidates:\n        if candidate[\"concept_id\"] not in seen:\n            seen[candidate[\"concept_id\"]] = candidate\n\n    all_candidates = list(seen.values())\n\n    if not all_candidates:\n        logger.debug(\n            \"No OMOP candidates found for '%s' in domains %s\",\n            entity_text[:50],\n            domain_ids,\n        )\n        return OmopLookupResult()\n\n    # Score and sort\n    scored = _score_candidates(entity_text, all_candidates)\n\n    # Apply primary domain bonus\n    primary_domain = domain_ids[0] if domain_ids else None\n    if primary_domain:\n        for candidate in scored:\n            if candidate.get(\"domain_id\") == primary_domain:\n                candidate[\"score\"] = min(\n                    candidate[\"score\"] + PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS, 1.0\n                )\n        # Re-sort after bonus\n        scored.sort(key=lambda c: c[\"score\"], reverse=True)\n\n    best = scored[0]\n\n    if best[\"score\"] < OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE:\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Best OMOP candidate for '%s' scored %.3f \"\n            \"(below threshold %.2f) — skipping\",\n            entity_text[:50],\n            best[\"score\"],\n            OMOP_MIN_MATCH_SCORE,\n        )\n        return OmopLookupResult()\n\n    logger.info(\n        \"OMOP match for '%s': concept_id=%s, name='%s', score=%.3f, method=%s\",\n        entity_text[:50],\n        best[\"concept_id\"],\n        best[\"concept_name\"][:60],\n        best[\"score\"],\n        best[\"match_method\"],\n    )\n\n    return OmopLookupResult(\n        omop_concept_id=best[\"concept_id\"],\n        omop_concept_name=best[\"concept_name\"],\n        omop_vocabulary_id=best[\"vocabulary_id\"],\n        omop_domain_id=best[\"domain_id\"],\n        match_score=best[\"score\"],\n        match_method=best[\"match_method\"],\n    )"
}

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py",
  "old_string": "async def lookup_omop_concept(entity_text: str, entity_type: str) -> OmopLookupResult:\n    \"\"\"Look up the best OMOP standard concept for a medical entity.\n\n    Maps the entity type to an OMOP domain filter, queries the concept\n    and concept_synonym tables in the OMOP vocabulary database, scores\n    candidates with fuzzy string matching, and returns the best result\n    above the minimum similarity threshold.\n\n    The synchronous database I/O is executed in a thread-pool executor to\n    avoid blocking the async event loop.\n\n    Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL to be set. Raises RuntimeError if not configured.\n\n    Args:\n        entity_text: The medical entity text to look up (e.g. \"metformin\",\n            \"type 2 diabetes mellitus\", \"hemoglobin A1c\").\n        entity_type: Pipeline entity type used to determine the OMOP domain\n            filter. One of \"Condition\", \"Medication\", \"Lab_Value\",\n            \"Procedure\", \"Demographic\". Unknown types fall back to\n            \"Observation\".\n\n    Returns:\n        OmopLookupResult with the best-matching OMOP concept, or an empty\n        result if no match is found above the similarity threshold.\n\n    Raises:\n        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not set.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():\n        raise ValueError(\"Empty entity_text passed to lookup_omop_concept\")\n\n    domain_id = _get_domain_filter(entity_type)\n\n    logger.debug(\n        \"OMOP lookup: entity='%s', type='%s', domain='%s'\",\n        entity_text[:50],\n        entity_type,\n        domain_id,\n    )\n\n    result = await asyncio.to_thread(_sync_lookup, entity_text, domain_id)\n\n    return result",
  "new_string": "async def lookup_omop_concept(\n    entity_text: str,\n    entity_type: str,\n    *,\n    is_acronym: bool = False,\n) -> OmopLookupResult:\n    \"\"\"Look up the best OMOP standard concept for a medical entity.\n\n    Maps the entity type to OMOP domain filters (primary + fallback),\n    queries the concept and concept_synonym tables in the OMOP vocabulary\n    database, scores candidates with fuzzy string matching, and returns\n    the best result above the minimum similarity threshold.\n\n    The synchronous database I/O is executed in a thread-pool executor to\n    avoid blocking the async event loop.\n\n    Requires OMOP_VOCAB_URL to be set. Raises RuntimeError if not configured.\n\n    Args:\n        entity_text: The medical entity text to look up (e.g. \"metformin\",\n            \"type 2 diabetes mellitus\", \"hemoglobin A1c\").\n        entity_type: Pipeline entity type used to determine the OMOP domain\n            filter. One of \"Condition\", \"Medication\", \"Lab_Value\",\n            \"Procedure\", \"Demographic\". Unknown types fall back to\n            \"Observation\".\n        is_acronym: If True, skip domain filter for synonym search\n            to find acronyms across all OMOP domains.\n\n    Returns:\n        OmopLookupResult with the best-matching OMOP concept, or an empty\n        result if no match is found above the similarity threshold.\n\n    Raises:\n        RuntimeError: If OMOP_VOCAB_URL is not set.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not entity_text or not entity_text.strip():\n        raise ValueError(\"Empty entity_text passed to lookup_omop_concept\")\n\n    domain_ids = _get_domain_filters(entity_type)\n\n    logger.debug(\n        \"OMOP lookup: entity='%s', type='%s', domains=%s, acronym=%s\",\n        entity_text[:50],\n        entity_type,\n        domain_ids,\n        is_acronym,\n    )\n\n    result = await asyncio.to_thread(\n        _sync_lookup, entity_text, domain_ids, is_acronym=is_acronym\n    )\n\n    return result"
}

{

  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/terminology_router.py",
  "old_string": "logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Default routing config path relative to this file\n_DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"config\" / \"routing.yaml\"",
  "new_string": "logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Default routing config path relative to this file\n_DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = Path(__file__).parent.parent / \"config\" / \"routing.yaml\"\n\n# Short words that look like acronyms (all-caps, <=6 chars) but are not.\n_ACRONYM_ALLOWLIST = frozenset({\n    \"age\", \"sex\", \"bmi\", \"ast\", \"alt\", \"got\", \"gpt\",\n})\n\n\ndef _is_likely_acronym(text: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Check if text is likely a medical acronym.\n\n    Returns True if the text is short (<=6 chars), all uppercase, no spaces,\n    and not a known short word on the allowlist.\n\n    Pure string check — zero cost.\n\n    Args:\n        text: The entity text to check.\n\n    Returns:\n        True if the text appears to be a medical acronym.\n    \"\"\"\n    stripped = text.strip()\n    if not stripped or \" \" in stripped:\n        return False\n    if len(stripped) > 6:\n        return False\n    if stripped.upper() != stripped:\n        return False\n    if stripped.lower() in _ACRONYM_ALLOWLIST:\n        return False\n    return True"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/terminology_router.py",
  "old_string": "    async def route_entity(\n        self, entity_text: str, entity_type: str\n    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:\n        \"\"\"Route entity to all matching APIs and return all candidates.\n\n        Queries each API configured for the entity type via ToolUniverse SDK.\n        Individual API failures are logged and skipped (error accumulation).\n        The caller (MedGemma ground node) receives all available candidates\n        and selects the best match.\n\n        Args:\n            entity_text: The entity text to look up (e.g. \"metformin\").\n            entity_type: The entity type (e.g. \"Medication\").\n\n        Returns:\n            List of GroundingCandidate objects from all queried APIs.\n            Empty list if entity type is skipped or all APIs fail.\n        \"\"\"\n        api_names = self.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)\n\n        if not api_names:\n            return []",
  "new_string": "    async def route_entity(\n        self, entity_text: str, entity_type: str\n    ) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:\n        \"\"\"Route entity to all matching APIs and return all candidates.\n\n        Queries each API configured for the entity type via ToolUniverse SDK.\n        Individual API failures are logged and skipped (error accumulation).\n        The caller (MedGemma ground node) receives all available candidates\n        and selects the best match.\n\n        For likely acronyms, UMLS is always included in the API list\n        regardless of entity type configuration (UMLS has the broadest\n        abbreviation coverage via AUI atom-level synonyms).\n\n        Args:\n            entity_text: The entity text to look up (e.g. \"metformin\").\n            entity_type: The entity type (e.g. \"Medication\").\n\n        Returns:\n            List of GroundingCandidate objects from all queried APIs.\n            Empty list if entity type is skipped or all APIs fail.\n        \"\"\"\n        api_names = self.get_apis_for_entity(entity_type)\n\n        if not api_names:\n            return []\n\n        # Smart acronym routing: always include UMLS for acronyms\n        if _is_likely_acronym(entity_text) and \"umls\" not in api_names:\n            api_names = [*api_names, \"umls\"]\n            logger.info(\n                \"Acronym detected ('%s') — adding UMLS to routing \"\n                \"for broader abbreviation coverage\",\n                entity_text,\n            )"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py",
  "old_string": "async def _ground_entity_parallel(",
  "new_string": "def _ground_categorical_values(\n    mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],\n) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:\n    \"\"\"Ground categorical values in field mappings using unit_normalizer.\n\n    For standard-type mappings with non-numeric values (e.g. \"Severe\",\n    \"positive\", \"NYHA Class III\"), attempt to resolve a value_concept_id\n    using the unit_normalizer's value lookup.\n\n    This is a best-effort post-processing step — failures don't block\n    the grounding result.\n\n    Args:\n        mappings: List of field mapping dicts from generate_field_mappings.\n\n    Returns:\n        The same list with value_concept_id populated where possible.\n    \"\"\"\n    _NUMERIC_RE = re.compile(r\"^[\\d.,\\-]+$\")\n\n    for mapping in mappings:\n        try:\n            value_obj = mapping.get(\"value\", {})\n            if not isinstance(value_obj, dict):\n                continue\n\n            # Only process standard-type values\n            if value_obj.get(\"type\") != \"standard\":\n                continue\n\n            val_text = value_obj.get(\"value\")\n            if not val_text or not isinstance(val_text, str):\n                continue\n\n            # Skip numeric values — they don't need concept grounding\n            if _NUMERIC_RE.match(val_text.strip()):\n                continue\n\n            # Already grounded\n            if mapping.get(\"value_concept_id\"):\n                continue\n\n            # Try unit_normalizer's categorical value lookup (fast path)\n            normalized_text, concept_id = normalize_value(val_text)\n            if concept_id is not None:\n                mapping[\"value_concept_id\"] = str(concept_id)\n                mapping[\"value_concept_system\"] = \"OMOP\"\n                logger.debug(\n                    \"Grounded categorical value '%s' → concept_id=%s\",\n                    val_text,\n                    concept_id,\n                )\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\n                \"Categorical value grounding failed for mapping: %s\", e\n            )\n\n    return mappings\n\n\nasync def _ground_entity_parallel("
}
```

All 6 checks pass. Here is a summary of what was written and why each decision was made.

---

## `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/scripts/validate_grounding_fixes.py`

The script runs 6 independent validation checks and prints `[PASS]`/`[FAIL]` for each, then exits with `sys.exit(1)` if any failed.

### Key implementation details

**Path setup** — The protocol-processor-service `src/` directory is prepended to `sys.path` so that `protocol_processor.*` imports resolve without requiring an editable install. The repo root is derived from the script's own `__file__` path via `pathlib.Path.resolve()`, making it portable regardless of where `uv run` is invoked from.

**Check 1 — Decomposition prompt** — Reads `entity_decompose.jinja2` with `Path.read_text()` and asserts both `"Do NOT separate anatomical modifiers"` and `"tricompartmental knee replacement"` are present (both confirmed in the actual file).

**Check 2 — Relation validation** — Imports `FieldMappingItem` from `protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper`. Tests that `"="`, `"!="`, `">"`, `"contains"` pass unchanged; that `"has"→"contains"`, `"is"→"="`, `"=="→"="` are normalized by the `@field_validator("relation", mode="before")` hook; and that `"has_value"` (not in `_RELATION_MAP` and not a valid `RelationOperator` literal) raises `pydantic.ValidationError`.

**Check 3 — Acronym detection** — Imports the private `_is_likely_acronym` function from `protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router` and verifies it returns `True` for `["HTN", "MI", "DM", "CKD", "COPD"]` and `False` for `["age", "metformin", "body mass index", "sex"]`. `"age"` and `"sex"` are on the `_ACRONYM_ALLOWLIST`; multi-word strings fail the `" " in stripped` guard; lowercase strings fail the `stripped.upper() != stripped` guard.

**Check 4 — Multi-domain OMOP config** — Imports `ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAINS` from `protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper` and asserts the three required mappings: `Condition→["Condition","Observation"]`, `Lab_Value→["Measurement","Observation"]`, `Procedure→["Procedure","Observation"]`.

**Check 5 — Grounding system prompt** — Reads `grounding_system.jinja2` and asserts `"DO NOT retry the exact same phrase"` is present.

**Check 6 — Grounding reasoning prompt** — Reads `grounding_reasoning.jinja2` and asserts `"SUBSTANTIALLY DIFFERENT, SIMPLER query"` is present.

All 36 tests pass. Here is a summary of what was written and how each test is structured.

---

**File written:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/tests/test_grounding_fixes.py`

**Result:** 36 tests collected, 36 passed in 0.13s.

---

**Test breakdown by class:**

**`TestRelationLiteralRejectsInvalid`** (1 test)
Constructs a `FieldMappingItem` with `relation="has_value"` and asserts `pydantic.ValidationError` is raised, since `"has_value"` is not in the `RelationOperator` Literal set and `_normalize_relation` has no mapping for it.

**`TestRelationNormalizerMapsLegacy`** (5 tests, parametrized)
Uses `@pytest.mark.parametrize` to test all five legacy-to-canonical mappings (`has→contains`, `is→=`, `==→=`, `not→not_contains`, `range→within`). Each constructs a valid `FieldMappingItem` to confirm the `@field_validator` runs before Literal validation.

**`TestIsLikelyAcronym`** (12 tests, parametrized)
Tests `_is_likely_acronym` from `protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router`. Six True cases (HTN, MI, CKD, COPD, DM, AF) and six False cases including allowlist entries (BMI→"bmi", AST→"ast") and strings with spaces or lowercase characters.

**`TestAcronymRoutingAddsUmls`** (3 tests)
Uses a `tmp_path` fixture to write a custom `routing.yaml` with `TestType: [icd10]` (no umls). Verifies baseline, verifies `_is_likely_acronym("HTN")` is True, then patches `_query_tooluniverse` on the router instance and calls `route_entity("HTN", "TestType")` — asserts `"umls"` appears in the called API names.

**`TestOmopAcronymDropsDomainFilter`** (1 test)
Patches `_get_omop_engine`, `_query_concept_table`, and `_query_synonym_table`. Calls `_sync_lookup("HTN", [...], is_acronym=True)` and asserts `_query_synonym_table` was called with `skip_domain_filter=True` in kwargs.

**`TestOmopMultiDomainQuery`** (1 test)
Same three patches. Calls `_sync_lookup("pregnancy", ["Condition", "Observation"])` and asserts `_query_concept_table` received `domain_ids=["Condition", "Observation"]` as its third positional argument.

**`TestOmopPrimaryDomainScoredHigher`** (3 tests)
One integration test: patches the DB layer, returns two candidates with identical `match_text` but different `domain_id` ("Condition" vs "Observation"), and asserts `_sync_lookup` returns the Condition domain concept_id (because `PRIMARY_DOMAIN_BONUS` lifts it). Two unit tests verify the constant value is 0.05 and that `_score_candidates` itself does not apply the bonus (so both candidates have equal scores before `_sync_lookup` applies it).

**`TestDecomposeKeepsCompoundConcepts`** (3 tests) and **`TestDecomposeSplitsComorbidities`** (3 tests)
Read the template at `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/entity_decompose.jinja2` and check string containment for the key phrases from the prompt rules.

**`TestValueConceptFieldsOnFieldMappingItem`** (4 tests)
Verifies `FieldMappingItem` accepts `value_concept_id` and `value_concept_system` as optional string fields, both individually and together, and that they default to `None` when omitted.

## Implementation Complete — All 6 Grounding Fixes

### Fix 1: Over-Decomposition of Complex Concepts
- **`prompts/entity_decompose.jinja2`** — Added compound concept rules with explicit DO NOT / DO examples (e.g., keep "tricompartmental knee replacement" together, split "Type 2 diabetes with nephropathy")

### Fix 2: Hallucinated Relations in Field Mappings
- **`tools/field_mapper.py`** — Changed `relation: str` → `relation: RelationOperator` (Literal type with 10 valid operators). Added `@field_validator("relation", mode="before")` that normalizes LLM outputs ("has"→"contains", "is"→"=", etc.) before Literal validation rejects invalid ones.

### Fix 3: Categorical Values Not Grounded
- **`tools/field_mapper.py`** — Added `value_concept_id` and `value_concept_system` optional fields to `FieldMappingItem`
- **`nodes/ground.py`** — Added `_ground_categorical_values()` post-processing that uses `normalize_value()` to resolve categorical text values to OMOP concept IDs

### Fix 4: Retry Loop Wastes Steps
- **`prompts/grounding_system.jinja2`** — Added "Search Strategy" section with progressive simplification rules
- **`prompts/grounding_reasoning.jinja2`** — Strengthened Q3 with "MUST suggest a SUBSTANTIALLY DIFFERENT, SIMPLER query" instruction and ordered simplification strategies

### Fix 5: Overly Restrictive OMOP Domain Filtering
- **`tools/omop_mapper.py`** — Added `ENTITY_TYPE_TO_OMOP_DOMAINS` multi-domain mapping (e.g., Condition→[Condition, Observation]). Updated SQL queries to use `IN` clause. Primary domain candidates get a 0.05 scoring bonus.

### Fix 6: Acronym Detection and Smart Routing
- **`tools/terminology_router.py`** — Added `_is_likely_acronym()` heuristic (zero-cost string check). Acronyms automatically get UMLS added to routing.
- **`tools/omop_mapper.py`** — Acronyms skip domain filter in synonym search (search all domains).
- **`nodes/ground.py`** — Passes acronym flag to OMOP lookup.

Now I have a complete picture. Here is the full analysis:

---

## MLflow Tracing: Complete Configuration Analysis

### 1. How MLflow is Initialized / Configured

There are two initialization points, and they have a subtle conflict:

**Primary initialization — `api_service/main.py` `lifespan()` (runs at API startup in the async event loop):**
```python
# main.py lines 56-80
mlflow.set_tracking_uri(tracking_uri)
mlflow.set_experiment("protocol-processing")   # fixed name "protocol-processing"
```

**Secondary initialization — `trigger.py` `_ensure_mlflow()` (dead code path — never called):**
```python
# trigger.py lines 106-124 — _ensure_mlflow() creates a timestamped experiment name
mlflow.set_experiment(f"protocol-processing-{ts}")  # e.g. "protocol-processing-20260224-153000"
```

**Critical conflict**: `_ensure_mlflow()` is defined but is NO LONGER CALLED anywhere (the recent diff shows it was removed from `_run_pipeline`). The comment in `_run_pipeline` explicitly says "Do NOT call `_ensure_mlflow()` here — it would overwrite the global `_active_experiment_id` set by `main.py`."

So in practice:
- `main.py` sets experiment = `"protocol-processing"` at startup
- All pipeline traces go to that experiment
- `_ensure_mlflow()` is orphaned dead code

### 2. How Tracing Works in the Pipeline (`tracing.py`)

**Architecture**: Each pipeline node creates its own independent top-level MLflow trace (NOT a child span), using `pipeline_span()` as a context manager.

```python
# tracing.py — the key gate
if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
    with mlflow.start_span(name=name, span_type=span_type) as span:
        tags = {"node": name}
        if protocol_id: tags["protocol_id"] = protocol_id
        run_id = _pipeline_run_id.get()       # ContextVar set in _run_pipeline()
        if run_id: tags["run_id"] = run_id
        mlflow.update_current_trace(tags=tags)
        yield span
        return
# Falls through to _NoOpSpan if MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI is not set
yield _NoOpSpan()
```

**Nodes that use `pipeline_span()`:**
- `parse_node` (parse.py line 55)
- `ground_node` (ground.py lines 537-539)
- Likely all other pipeline nodes (ingest, extract, persist — not fully read but pattern is consistent)

**`run_id` propagation** (recently added in this branch):
1. `trigger._run_pipeline()` calls `set_pipeline_run_id(thread_id)` where `thread_id = "{protocol_id}:{uuid4}"`
2. This sets a `contextvars.ContextVar` (`_pipeline_run_id`)
3. Every node's `pipeline_span()` reads it and tags its trace with `run_id`
4. **Potential issue**: `asyncio.run()` creates a new event loop with a *copied* context from the thread. The ContextVar is set inside `_run_pipeline` which runs *inside* `asyncio.run()`, so the var is set before `graph.ainvoke()` runs — this should work correctly.

**MLflow autolog is explicitly disabled** (major recent change):
- Before this branch: `mlflow.langchain.autolog(run_tracer_inline=True)` was called in `_run_pipeline()` and also in `lifespan()`
- Now: Both are removed. The diff shows the removal of 6 lines of autolog code from `main.py` and 2 lines from `_run_pipeline()`
- **Why**: Autolog wraps the entire `graph.ainvoke()` in a single trace that only appears *after the whole pipeline finishes*. The new approach creates per-node traces that appear in real-time.

### 3. Environment Variables Required

From `.env.example` (line 75) and `docker-compose.yml` (line 15):

| Variable | Local dev | Docker |
|---|---|---|
| `MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI` | `http://localhost:5001` (set by Makefile dynamically) | `http://mlflow:5000` |
| `MLFLOW_TRACE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` | not set | `300` (Docker only) |

**The single gate that enables ALL tracing:**
```python
# tracing.py line 72
if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
```
If this env var is absent or empty, every node silently falls back to `_NoOpSpan()`. No error is raised.

### 4. How the Pipeline is Triggered (`trigger.py`)

The flow:
1. `OutboxProcessor` (started in `main.py` `lifespan()`) polls the DB outbox table for events
2. On `protocol_uploaded` event → calls `handle_protocol_uploaded(payload)`
3. `handle_protocol_uploaded()` generates `thread_id = f"{protocol_id}:{uuid4()}"`, stores it in `protocol.metadata_["pipeline_thread_id"]`, then calls `asyncio.run(_run_pipeline(...))`
4. `_run_pipeline()` sets `set_pipeline_run_id(thread_id)`, then calls `graph.ainvoke()`
5. Each graph node wraps its work in `pipeline_span()` which creates an independent MLflow trace

**Note**: The outbox processor runs handlers via `run_in_executor` (in a thread), so `asyncio.run()` inside the handler is safe — there's no existing event loop in that thread.

**Orphan cleanup at startup**: `_cleanup_orphan_traces()` runs at module import time (trigger.py line 89), closing any `IN_PROGRESS` traces older than 1 hour from previous crashes.

### 5. How MLflow Server is Started

**Local dev (`make run-dev`):**
```bash
# Finds an available port starting from 5001
uv run mlflow server \
  --host 0.0.0.0 \
  --port $MLFLOW_PORT \
  --backend-store-uri sqlite:///.mlflow/mlflow.db \
  --artifacts-destination .mlflow/artifacts
# Sets MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI=http://localhost:$MLFLOW_PORT in the shell environment
```

**Docker (`docker-compose.yml`):**
```yaml
# Uses mlflow v3.9.0, exposes port 5001 → internal 5000
image: ghcr.io/mlflow/mlflow:v3.9.0
# api service gets: MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI=http://mlflow:5000
```

**`make run-api` (standalone)**: Loads `.env` but does NOT start MLflow. If `.env` has `MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI=http://localhost:5001` and MLflow isn't running separately, traces will fail silently (the env var is set, but the HTTP calls to the tracking server will fail, caught by the `except Exception` in `pipeline_span()`).

### 6. Root Causes Why Traces Might Not Appear

**Most likely causes (in priority order):**

**A. `MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI` not set in the process environment.**
The check at `tracing.py:72` is the single gate. Using `make run-api` without MLflow running (or without the env var exported) silently disables everything. The `.env.example` has the value but the actual `.env` must have it.

**B. MLflow server not running when using `make run-api` standalone.**
The env var is set but the HTTP connection fails. The `except Exception` in `pipeline_span()` catches connection errors and falls back to no-op, logging only a WARNING: `"MLflow span creation failed, falling back to no-op"`.

**C. Experiment mismatch between `main.py` and `trigger.py`.**
`main.py` sets experiment `"protocol-processing"`. The now-dead `_ensure_mlflow()` in `trigger.py` would have created a timestamped experiment name. If somehow `_ensure_mlflow()` got called (e.g., in a future change), traces would scatter across experiments. Currently not a problem since `_ensure_mlflow()` is unreachable.

**D. ContextVar not propagating across `asyncio.run()` boundary.**
`asyncio.run()` in `handle_protocol_uploaded()` creates a new event loop. `set_pipeline_run_id()` is called *inside* `_run_pipeline()` (which runs inside `asyncio.run()`), so the ContextVar is set in the correct async context. This is correct. However, if `set_pipeline_run_id()` were called *before* `asyncio.run()`, it would be set in the wrong context.

**E. The recent architectural change from autolog to per-node spans.**
The diff shows `mlflow.langchain.autolog()` was removed and replaced with per-node `pipeline_span()`. If you were looking at MLflow traces from before this change, you would have seen a different trace structure (one big trace per pipeline run instead of multiple per-node traces). After this change, you need to filter by `tags.run_id` to group traces from the same pipeline invocation.

**F. MLflow version compatibility.**
`mlflow>=3.8.1` is required (pyproject.toml line 62). The docker image uses `v3.9.0`. The `_cleanup_orphan_traces()` function has a `try/except (AttributeError, TypeError)` guard because `search_traces` API signature varies between MLflow 3.x versions.

### Quick Diagnosis Checklist

```bash
# 1. Is MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI set?
echo $MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI

# 2. Is MLflow server reachable?
curl http://localhost:5001/health

# 3. Does the experiment exist?
uv run python -c "import mlflow; mlflow.set_tracking_uri('http://localhost:5001'); print(mlflow.MlflowClient().get_experiment_by_name('protocol-processing'))"

# 4. Are there any traces?
uv run python scripts/verify_mlflow_tracing.py

# 5. Clear stale traces and start fresh
make mlflow-clear
```
