  "content": "# Plan: Replace Static YAML Lookups with Dynamic OMOP DB Queries\n\n## Context\n\nThe current `unit_normalizer.py` uses a static `ucum_mappings.yaml` file (297 lines) for three things:\n1. **Unit normalization**: Map clinical unit text (\"mg/dL\", \"years\") to UCUM codes + OMOP unit_concept_ids\n2. **Categorical value mapping**: Map qualifier words (\"positive\", \"negative\") to OMOP concept IDs\n3. **Ordinal scale grading**: Map ECOG/Karnofsky/NYHA/ASA grades to OMOP concept IDs\n\nThis is brittle — a misspelling, unseen alias, or new scale breaks it silently. The OMOP vocabulary DB already contains all of this data (UCUM concepts, SNOMED qualifiers, ordinal scale concepts). We should query it dynamically instead.\n\n---\n\n## Approach: DB-Backed Caches with Lazy Init\n\nReplace YAML reads with SQL queries against the existing OMOP vocabulary DB (already available via `OMOP_VOCAB_URL`). Use process-lifetime `lru_cache` so the DB is hit once per startup, not per-entity.\n\n### Key Principle\nThe OMOP `concept` table (vocabulary_id='UCUM') and `concept_synonym` table already contain all UCUM units and their aliases. The `concept` table also contains SNOMED qualifier values and ordinal scale concepts. We query these instead of maintaining a parallel YAML copy.\n\n---\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Add DB lookup functions to `omop_mapper.py`\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`\n\nAdd three new synchronous query functions (they run in the existing `_get_omop_engine()` context):\n\n```python\ndef lookup_ucum_unit(unit_text: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Query OMOP concept table for UCUM unit by name/synonym.\n\n    SELECT concept_id, concept_code FROM concept\n    WHERE vocabulary_id = 'UCUM' AND standard_concept = 'S'\n      AND (concept_name ILIKE :pattern OR concept_code ILIKE :pattern)\n    UNION\n    SELECT c.concept_id, c.concept_code FROM concept_synonym s\n    JOIN concept c ON s.concept_id = c.concept_id\n    WHERE c.vocabulary_id = 'UCUM' AND c.standard_concept = 'S'\n      AND s.concept_synonym_name ILIKE :pattern\n\n    Returns (ucum_code, omop_concept_id) or (None, None).\n    \"\"\"\n\ndef lookup_value_concept(value_text: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Query OMOP for SNOMED qualifier value concepts.\n\n    Searches concept table where vocabulary_id IN ('SNOMED', 'LOINC')\n    and domain_id = 'Meas Value' for terms like 'positive', 'negative'.\n\n    Returns (normalized_text, omop_concept_id) or (None, None).\n    \"\"\"\n\ndef lookup_ordinal_concept(scale_name: str, grade: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Query OMOP for an ordinal scale grade concept.\n\n    Searches concept + concept_synonym tables for the scale+grade\n    combination (e.g. \"ECOG 2\", \"Karnofsky 70\", \"NYHA III\").\n\n    Returns omop_concept_id or None.\n    \"\"\"\n```\n\n### 2. Rewrite `unit_normalizer.py` to use DB-backed caches\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py`\n\nReplace `_load_ucum_mappings()` YAML load with a lazy-init cache that calls `lookup_ucum_unit()`:\n\n```python\n@lru_cache(maxsize=512)\ndef normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Normalize unit text to UCUM code + OMOP concept_id.\n\n    Strategy:\n    1. Try exact match in OMOP concept table (vocabulary_id='UCUM')\n    2. Try synonym match in concept_synonym table\n    3. Return (None, None) if unrecognized\n    \"\"\"\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import lookup_ucum_unit\n    return lookup_ucum_unit(unit_text)\n```\n\nSimilarly for `normalize_value()`:\n```python\n@lru_cache(maxsize=128)\ndef normalize_value(value_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import lookup_value_concept\n    return lookup_value_concept(value_text)\n```\n\nFor ordinal scales, keep the **alias-to-scale mapping as a Python constant** (this is clinical knowledge about which names refer to the same scale — not in OMOP). But resolve grade→concept_id from DB:\n\n```python\n# Clinical knowledge: which names map to which scale (not in OMOP)\nORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"ecog\": \"ecog\", \"ecog ps\": \"ecog\", \"ecog performance status\": \"ecog\",\n    \"who performance status\": \"ecog\", \"zubrod score\": \"ecog\",\n    \"karnofsky\": \"karnofsky\", \"kps\": \"karnofsky\",\n    \"nyha\": \"nyha\", \"nyha class\": \"nyha\", \"nyha functional class\": \"nyha\",\n    \"asa\": \"asa\", \"asa physical status\": \"asa\", \"asa ps\": \"asa\",\n    # ... (extensible without YAML edits)\n}\n\ndef normalize_ordinal_value(value_text, entity_text):\n    scale_key = _match_ordinal_scale(entity_text)  # uses ORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES\n    if scale_key is None:\n        return None\n    # Resolve grade concept from DB instead of YAML\n    from protocol_processor.tools.omop_mapper import lookup_ordinal_concept\n    concept_id = lookup_ordinal_concept(scale_key, cleaned_value)\n    return (concept_id, 8527)  # 8527 = {score} unit\n```\n\n### 3. Add cache warming function\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py`\n\n```python\nasync def warm_caches() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Pre-warm the unit and value caches on startup.\n\n    Called from ground_node preflight. Queries common units/values\n    once so per-entity lookups hit the lru_cache.\n    \"\"\"\n```\n\nCall this from `ground.py` at the start of `_ground_entity_parallel()` (guarded by a module-level `_caches_warmed` flag).\n\n### 4. Graceful fallback when OMOP DB unavailable\n\nIf `OMOP_VOCAB_URL` is not set (dev/test environments), fall back to reading from `ucum_mappings.yaml` — don't break existing tests. The YAML becomes a fallback, not the primary source.\n\n```python\ndef normalize_unit(unit_text):\n    try:\n        return _db_lookup_unit(unit_text)\n    except RuntimeError:  # OMOP_VOCAB_URL not set\n        return _yaml_fallback_unit(unit_text)\n```\n\n### 5. Deprecate (don't delete) `ucum_mappings.yaml`\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/config/ucum_mappings.yaml`\n\nAdd a header comment: `# DEPRECATED: This file is a fallback. Primary lookups use OMOP vocabulary DB.`\n\nKeep the file for:\n- Offline/test environments without OMOP DB\n- Reference documentation of supported mappings\n\n---\n\n## Files Modified\n\n| File | Action |\n|------|--------|\n| `tools/omop_mapper.py` | Add `lookup_ucum_unit()`, `lookup_value_concept()`, `lookup_ordinal_concept()` |\n| `tools/unit_normalizer.py` | Rewrite to use DB-backed caches with YAML fallback |\n| `config/ucum_mappings.yaml` | Add deprecation header comment |\n| `nodes/ground.py` | Call `warm_caches()` on first grounding run |\n| `tests/test_unit_normalizer.py` | Update to mock DB functions instead of YAML |\n| `tests/test_grounding_fixes.py` | Add tests for DB lookup functions |\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\n# Lint + format\nuv run ruff check .\nuv run ruff format --check .\n\n# Type check\nuv run mypy services/protocol-processor-service/src\n\n# Unit tests (mock DB — no OMOP container needed)\nuv run pytest services/protocol-processor-service/tests/ -v\n\n# Integration test (requires OMOP container running)\nuv run pytest services/protocol-processor-service/tests/ -v -k \"omop\" --run-integration\n```\n\nManual validation: run `make run-dev`, process a protocol with known units (mg/dL, %, years) and ordinal scales (ECOG 2, ASA 3), verify OMOP concept_ids are populated in the grounding results.\n"
}

     1→"""E2E test configuration and fixtures.
     2→
     3→Provides Docker Compose stack detection, automatic skip when the stack is
     4→unavailable, and core fixtures (authenticated HTTP client, direct DB session,
     5→PDF upload factory, database cleanup) for end-to-end tests against the real
     6→running services.
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→    Mark any E2E test with ``@pytest.mark.e2e``. When the Docker Compose stack
    10→    is not running, all e2e-marked tests are automatically skipped (exit 0).
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import base64
    16→import logging
    17→import os
    18→import time
    19→from pathlib import Path
    20→from typing import Callable, Generator
    21→
    22→import httpx
    23→import jwt
    24→import pytest
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Default test PDF (smallest available at ~90K)
    29→_DEFAULT_TEST_PDF = "data/protocols/crc_protocols/isrctn/48616-d8fc1476.pdf"
    30→
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→# Stack availability detection
    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→
    35→_STACK_AVAILABLE: bool | None = None
    36→
    37→_DEFAULT_API_URL = "http://localhost:8000"
    38→_DEFAULT_DATABASE_URL = "postgresql://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/app"
    39→
    40→
    41→def _check_stack() -> bool:
    42→    """Check whether the Docker Compose stack (API + PostgreSQL) is reachable."""
    43→    api_url = os.getenv("E2E_API_URL", _DEFAULT_API_URL)
    44→    try:
    45→        resp = httpx.get(f"{api_url}/health", timeout=3.0)
    46→        if resp.status_code != 200:
    47→            return False
    48→    except (httpx.ConnectError, httpx.TimeoutException, Exception):
    49→        return False
    50→
    51→    # Also verify PostgreSQL is reachable
    52→    try:
    53→        from sqlalchemy import create_engine, text
    54→
    55→        db_url = os.getenv("E2E_DATABASE_URL", _DEFAULT_DATABASE_URL)
    56→        engine = create_engine(db_url, connect_args={"connect_timeout": 3})
    57→        with engine.connect() as conn:
    58→            conn.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
    59→        engine.dispose()
    60→    except Exception:
    61→        return False
    62→
    63→    return True
    64→
    65→
    66→def _wants_e2e(config: pytest.Config) -> bool:
    67→    """Return True only if the user explicitly requested e2e tests.
    68→
    69→    This guard prevents the health-check from adding latency to regular
    70→    ``uv run pytest`` invocations that do not target E2E tests.
    71→    """
    72→    # Check -m flag for e2e marker expression
    73→    markexpr: str = config.getoption("-m", default="")
    74→    if markexpr and "e2e" in markexpr:
    75→        return True
    76→
    77→    # Check if any explicit test path is under tests/e2e/
    78→    args: list[str] = config.getoption("file_or_dir", default=[]) or []
    79→    for arg in args:
    80→        if "tests/e2e" in str(arg) or "tests\\e2e" in str(arg):
    81→            return True
    82→
    83→    return False
    84→
    85→
    86→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    87→# Pytest hooks
    88→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    89→
    90→
    91→def pytest_configure(config: pytest.Config) -> None:
    92→    """Detect Docker Compose availability once per session (if e2e requested)."""
    93→    global _STACK_AVAILABLE
    94→    if _wants_e2e(config):
    95→        _STACK_AVAILABLE = _check_stack()
    96→    else:
    97→        # Don't waste time checking -- no e2e tests requested
    98→        _STACK_AVAILABLE = False
    99→
   100→
   101→def pytest_collection_modifyitems(
   102→    config: pytest.Config, items: list[pytest.Item]
   103→) -> None:
   104→    """Auto-skip all ``@pytest.mark.e2e`` tests when the stack is unavailable."""
   105→    if _STACK_AVAILABLE:
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    skip_marker = pytest.mark.skip(
   109→        reason=(
   110→            "Docker Compose stack not running "
   111→            "(API at localhost:8000 or PostgreSQL at localhost:5432 unreachable)"
   112→        )
   113→    )
   114→    for item in items:
   115→        if "e2e" in item.keywords:
   116→            item.add_marker(skip_marker)
   117→

   196→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   197→# Upload fixture (factory pattern)

   199→
   200→
   201→@pytest.fixture()
   202→def upload_test_pdf(
   203→    e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
   204→    _created_protocol_ids: list[str],
   205→) -> Callable[..., str]:
   206→    """Factory fixture: upload a real PDF through the API upload flow.
   207→
   208→    Returns a callable ``upload_test_pdf(pdf_path=None) -> protocol_id``.
   209→    The upload follows the same three-step flow the frontend uses:
   210→
   211→    1. POST /protocols/upload  (get signed/local upload URL)
   212→    2. PUT pdf bytes to the upload URL
   213→    3. POST /protocols/{id}/confirm-upload
   214→
   215→    Requires ``USE_LOCAL_STORAGE=1`` on the API container so that the
   216→    upload URL points to the local-upload endpoint.
   217→    """
   218→
   219→    def _upload(pdf_path: str | None = None) -> str:
   220→        path = Path(pdf_path or _DEFAULT_TEST_PDF)
   221→        if not path.is_absolute():
   222→            # Resolve relative to repo root
   223→            repo_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
   224→            path = repo_root / path
   225→        assert path.exists(), f"Test PDF not found: {path}"
   226→
   227→        pdf_bytes = path.read_bytes()
   228→
   229→        # Step 1: Request upload URL
   230→        resp = e2e_api_client.post(
   231→            "/protocols/upload",
   232→            json={
   233→                "filename": "test-e2e.pdf",
   234→                "content_type": "application/pdf",
   235→                "file_size_bytes": len(pdf_bytes),
   236→            },
   237→        )
   238→        assert resp.status_code == 200, (
   239→            f"Upload request failed ({resp.status_code}): {resp.text}"
   240→        )
   241→        data = resp.json()
   242→        protocol_id = data["protocol_id"]
   243→        upload_url = data["upload_url"]
   244→
   245→        # Step 2: PUT the PDF bytes to the upload URL
   246→        put_resp = httpx.put(
   247→            upload_url,
   248→            content=pdf_bytes,
   249→            headers={"Content-Type": "application/pdf"},
   250→            timeout=30.0,
   251→        )
   252→        assert put_resp.status_code == 200, (
   253→            f"PUT to upload URL failed ({put_resp.status_code}): {put_resp.text}"
   254→        )
   255→
   256→        # Step 3: Confirm upload (with base64 PDF for quality scoring)
   257→        pdf_b64 = base64.b64encode(pdf_bytes).decode("ascii")
   258→        confirm_resp = e2e_api_client.post(
   259→            f"/protocols/{protocol_id}/confirm-upload",
   260→            json={"pdf_bytes_base64": pdf_b64},
   261→        )
   262→        assert confirm_resp.status_code == 200, (
   263→            f"Confirm upload failed ({confirm_resp.status_code}): {confirm_resp.text}"
   264→        )
   265→
   266→        _created_protocol_ids.append(protocol_id)
   267→        logger.info("Uploaded test PDF -> protocol_id=%s", protocol_id)
   268→        return protocol_id
   269→
   270→    return _upload

   273→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   274→# Database cleanup (autouse for all tests in tests/e2e/)
   275→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   276→
   277→
   278→@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
   279→def e2e_cleanup(
   280→    e2e_db_session,  # noqa: ANN001
   281→    _created_protocol_ids: list[str],
   282→):
   283→    """Delete all test-created protocols and related data after each test.
   284→
   285→    Deletion order respects foreign key constraints:
   286→    entities -> reviews -> audit_log -> criteria -> criteria_batches ->
   287→    outbox_events -> protocol
   288→    """
   289→    yield  # Run the test
   290→
   291→    if not _created_protocol_ids:
   292→        return
   293→
   294→    from sqlmodel import col, delete, select
   295→
   296→    from shared.models import (
   297→        AuditLog,
   298→        Criteria,
   299→        CriteriaBatch,
   300→        Entity,
   301→        OutboxEvent,
   302→        Protocol,
   303→        Review,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→    session = e2e_db_session
   307→
   308→    for protocol_id in _created_protocol_ids:
   309→        # Gather batch IDs for this protocol
   310→        batch_ids: list[str] = [
   311→            row
   312→            for row in session.exec(
   313→                select(CriteriaBatch.id).where(CriteriaBatch.protocol_id == protocol_id)
   314→            ).all()
   315→        ]
   316→
   317→        # Gather criteria IDs across all batches
   318→        criteria_ids: list[str] = []
   319→        if batch_ids:
   320→            criteria_ids = [
   321→                row
   322→                for row in session.exec(
   323→                    select(Criteria.id).where(col(Criteria.batch_id).in_(batch_ids))
   324→                ).all()
   325→            ]
   326→
   327→        # 1. Delete entities for these criteria
   328→        if criteria_ids:
   329→            session.exec(
   330→                delete(Entity).where(col(Entity.criteria_id).in_(criteria_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   331→            )
   332→
   333→        # 2. Delete reviews for these criteria
   334→        if criteria_ids:
   335→            session.exec(
   336→                delete(Review).where(col(Review.target_id).in_(criteria_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   337→            )
   338→
   339→        # 3. Delete audit log entries related to this protocol or its criteria
   340→        target_ids = [protocol_id, *criteria_ids]
   341→        session.exec(
   342→            delete(AuditLog).where(col(AuditLog.target_id).in_(target_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   343→        )
   344→
   345→        # 4. Delete criteria
   346→        if batch_ids:
   347→            session.exec(
   348→                delete(Criteria).where(col(Criteria.batch_id).in_(batch_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   349→            )
   350→
   351→        # 5. Delete criteria batches
   352→        if batch_ids:
   353→            session.exec(
   354→                delete(CriteriaBatch).where(col(CriteriaBatch.id).in_(batch_ids))  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   355→            )
   356→
   357→        # 6. Delete outbox events for this protocol
   358→        session.exec(
   359→            delete(OutboxEvent).where(
   360→                OutboxEvent.aggregate_id == protocol_id  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   361→            )
   362→        )
   363→
   364→        # 7. Delete the protocol itself
   365→        session.exec(
   366→            delete(Protocol).where(Protocol.id == protocol_id)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
   367→        )
   368→
   369→    session.commit()
   370→    logger.info(
   371→        "E2E cleanup: deleted %d protocol(s) and related data",
   372→        len(_created_protocol_ids),
   373→    )
   374→
   375→
   376→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   377→# Pipeline wait utility
   378→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   379→
   380→_TERMINAL_STATUSES = frozenset(
   381→    {
   382→        "pending_review",
   383→        "complete",
   384→        "extraction_failed",
   385→        "grounding_failed",
   386→        "pipeline_failed",
   387→        "dead_letter",
   388→    }
   389→)
   390→
   391→
   392→def wait_for_pipeline(
   393→    api_client: httpx.Client,
   394→    protocol_id: str,
   395→    timeout: int = 180,
   396→    poll_interval: int = 5,
   397→) -> dict:
   398→    """Poll protocol status until it reaches a terminal state.
   399→
   400→    Args:
   401→        api_client: Authenticated httpx client.
   402→        protocol_id: Protocol to monitor.
   403→        timeout: Maximum seconds to wait.
   404→        poll_interval: Seconds between polls.
   405→
   406→    Returns:
   407→        The final protocol response dict.
   408→
   409→    Raises:
   410→        TimeoutError: If timeout is exceeded before reaching terminal state.
   411→    """
   412→    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
   413→    while True:
   414→        resp = api_client.get(f"/protocols/{protocol_id}")
   415→        resp.raise_for_status()
   416→        data = resp.json()
   417→
   418→        status = data.get("status", "")
   419→        if status in _TERMINAL_STATUSES:
   420→            logger.info(
   421→                "Pipeline reached terminal status '%s' for %s",
   422→                status,
   423→                protocol_id,
   424→            )
   425→            return data
   426→
   427→        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
   428→            raise TimeoutError(
   429→                f"Protocol {protocol_id} still in '{status}' after {timeout}s"
   430→            )
   431→
   432→        time.sleep(poll_interval)
   433→

     1→"""Full pipeline E2E tests: upload PDF -> extraction -> grounding -> criteria with entities.
     2→
     3→Tests cover:
     4→- E2E-01: Pipeline completes to pending_review status
     5→- E2E-02: Extracted criteria include both inclusion and exclusion types
     6→- E2E-03: Entities are grounded with non-zero confidence and coding references
     7→- E2E-06: Numeric regression baseline thresholds are met
     8→
     9→Each test uploads its own PDF (independent, no shared state). The cleanup
    10→fixture in conftest.py is autouse and handles deletion after each test.
    11→
    12→Requires:
    13→- Docker Compose stack running (API + PostgreSQL + protocol-processor)
    14→- Real Gemini API access (GOOGLE_API_KEY or ADC configured)
    15→- Test PDF at data/protocols/crc_protocols/isrctn/48616-d8fc1476.pdf
    16→"""
    17→
    18→from __future__ import annotations
    19→
    20→from typing import Callable
    21→
    22→import httpx
    23→import pytest
    24→
    25→from .baseline import get_baseline
    26→from .conftest import wait_for_pipeline
    27→
    28→# Default test PDF (smallest CRC protocol, ~90K)
    29→_DEFAULT_PDF = "data/protocols/crc_protocols/isrctn/48616-d8fc1476.pdf"
    30→
    31→_FAILURE_STATUSES = frozenset(
    32→    {"extraction_failed", "grounding_failed", "pipeline_failed", "dead_letter"}
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    37→# Shared helpers (module-level, not fixtures)
    38→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    39→
    40→
    41→def _upload_and_wait(
    42→    upload_test_pdf: Callable[..., str],
    43→    e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
    44→    pdf_path: str | None = None,
    45→) -> tuple[str, dict]:
    46→    """Upload a PDF and wait for the pipeline to reach a terminal status.
    47→
    48→    Args:
    49→        upload_test_pdf: Factory fixture that uploads a PDF and returns protocol_id.
    50→        e2e_api_client: Authenticated httpx client.
    51→        pdf_path: Optional path to a specific PDF. Uses default if None.
    52→
    53→    Returns:
    54→        Tuple of (protocol_id, protocol_data dict).
    55→
    56→    Raises:
    57→        AssertionError: If pipeline reaches a failure status instead of pending_review.
    58→    """
    59→    protocol_id = upload_test_pdf(pdf_path=pdf_path or _DEFAULT_PDF)
    60→    protocol_data = wait_for_pipeline(e2e_api_client, protocol_id, timeout=180)
    61→
    62→    status = protocol_data.get("status", "")
    63→    assert status not in _FAILURE_STATUSES, (
    64→        f"Pipeline failed with status '{status}' for protocol {protocol_id}"
    65→    )
    66→    assert status == "pending_review", (
    67→        f"Expected 'pending_review' but got '{status}' for protocol {protocol_id}"
    68→    )
    69→
    70→    return protocol_id, protocol_data
    71→
    72→
    73→def _fetch_criteria(
    74→    e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
    75→    protocol_id: str,
    76→) -> list[dict]:
    77→    """Fetch criteria for a protocol by retrieving its first batch.
    78→
    79→    Args:
    80→        e2e_api_client: Authenticated httpx client.
    81→        protocol_id: Protocol to fetch criteria for.
    82→
    83→    Returns:
    84→        List of criterion dicts (each with entities list).
    85→
    86→    Raises:
    87→        AssertionError: If no batches or no criteria found.
    88→    """
    89→    # Get batches for the protocol
    90→    batches_resp = e2e_api_client.get(f"/protocols/{protocol_id}/batches")
    91→    assert batches_resp.status_code == 200, (
    92→        f"Failed to fetch batches ({batches_resp.status_code}): {batches_resp.text}"
    93→    )
    94→    batches = batches_resp.json()
    95→    assert len(batches) > 0, f"No batches found for protocol {protocol_id}"
    96→
    97→    batch_id = batches[0]["id"]
    98→
    99→    # Get criteria for the first batch
   100→    criteria_resp = e2e_api_client.get(f"/reviews/batches/{batch_id}/criteria")
   101→    assert criteria_resp.status_code == 200, (
   102→        f"Failed to fetch criteria ({criteria_resp.status_code}): {criteria_resp.text}"
   103→    )
   104→    criteria = criteria_resp.json()
   105→    assert len(criteria) > 0, (
   106→        f"No criteria found in batch {batch_id} for protocol {protocol_id}"
   107→    )
   108→
   109→    return criteria
   110→
   111→
   112→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   113→# Test class
   114→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   115→
   116→
   117→@pytest.mark.e2e
   118→class TestFullPipeline:
   119→    """E2E tests for the full upload-to-criteria-with-entities pipeline."""
   120→
   121→    def test_pipeline_completes_successfully(
   122→        self,
   123→        upload_test_pdf: Callable[..., str],
   124→        e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
   125→    ) -> None:
   126→        """E2E-01: Pipeline reaches pending_review status after uploading a real PDF."""
   127→        _protocol_id, protocol_data = _upload_and_wait(upload_test_pdf, e2e_api_client)
   128→        assert protocol_data["status"] == "pending_review"
   129→
   130→    def test_criteria_have_inclusion_and_exclusion(
   131→        self,
   132→        upload_test_pdf: Callable[..., str],
   133→        e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
   134→    ) -> None:
   135→        """E2E-02: Extracted criteria include both inclusion and exclusion types."""
   136→        protocol_id, _data = _upload_and_wait(upload_test_pdf, e2e_api_client)
   137→        criteria = _fetch_criteria(e2e_api_client, protocol_id)
   138→
   139→        baseline = get_baseline(_DEFAULT_PDF)
   140→
   141→        # Collect criteria types
   142→        types = [c["criteria_type"] for c in criteria]
   143→        inclusion_count = sum(1 for t in types if t == "inclusion")
   144→        exclusion_count = sum(1 for t in types if t == "exclusion")
   145→
   146→        assert "inclusion" in types, (
   147→            f"No inclusion criteria found. Types present: {set(types)}"
   148→        )
   149→        assert "exclusion" in types, (
   150→            f"No exclusion criteria found. Types present: {set(types)}"
   151→        )
   152→        assert len(criteria) >= baseline["min_criteria"], (
   153→            f"Total criteria ({len(criteria)}) below baseline ({baseline['min_criteria']})"
   154→        )
   155→        assert inclusion_count >= baseline["min_inclusion"], (
   156→            f"Inclusion count ({inclusion_count}) below baseline ({baseline['min_inclusion']})"
   157→        )
   158→        assert exclusion_count >= baseline["min_exclusion"], (
   159→            f"Exclusion count ({exclusion_count}) below baseline ({baseline['min_exclusion']})"
   160→        )
   161→
   162→    def test_entities_grounded_with_confidence(
   163→        self,
   164→        upload_test_pdf: Callable[..., str],
   165→        e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
   166→    ) -> None:
   167→        """E2E-03: Entities are grounded with non-zero confidence and coding refs."""
   168→        protocol_id, _data = _upload_and_wait(upload_test_pdf, e2e_api_client)
   169→        criteria = _fetch_criteria(e2e_api_client, protocol_id)
   170→
   171→        baseline = get_baseline(_DEFAULT_PDF)
   172→
   173→        # Collect all entities from all criteria
   174→        all_entities = []
   175→        for criterion in criteria:
   176→            all_entities.extend(criterion.get("entities", []))
   177→
   178→        assert len(all_entities) >= baseline["min_entities"], (
   179→            f"Total entities ({len(all_entities)}) below baseline ({baseline['min_entities']})"
   180→        )
   181→
   182→        # Filter grounded entities (non-null, non-zero confidence)
   183→        grounded = [
   184→            e
   185→            for e in all_entities
   186→            if e.get("grounding_confidence") is not None
   187→            and e["grounding_confidence"] > 0
   188→        ]
   189→        assert len(grounded) >= baseline["min_grounded_entities"], (
   190→            f"Grounded entities ({len(grounded)}) below baseline "
   191→            f"({baseline['min_grounded_entities']})"
   192→        )
   193→
   194→        # At least one grounded entity should have a coding reference
   195→        has_coding_ref = any(
   196→            e.get("umls_cui") is not None or e.get("snomed_code") is not None
   197→            for e in grounded
   198→        )
   199→        assert has_coding_ref, (
   200→            "No grounded entity has a non-null umls_cui or snomed_code"
   201→        )
   202→
   203→    def test_regression_baseline(
   204→        self,
   205→        upload_test_pdf: Callable[..., str],
   206→        e2e_api_client: httpx.Client,
   207→    ) -> None:
   208→        """E2E-06: All numeric regression baseline thresholds are met."""
   209→        protocol_id, _data = _upload_and_wait(upload_test_pdf, e2e_api_client)
   210→        criteria = _fetch_criteria(e2e_api_client, protocol_id)
   211→
   212→        baseline = get_baseline(_DEFAULT_PDF)
   213→
   214→        # Compute actual counts
   215→        types = [c["criteria_type"] for c in criteria]
   216→        inclusion_count = sum(1 for t in types if t == "inclusion")
   217→        exclusion_count = sum(1 for t in types if t == "exclusion")
   218→
   219→        all_entities = []
   220→        for criterion in criteria:
   221→            all_entities.extend(criterion.get("entities", []))
   222→
   223→        grounded = [
   224→            e
   225→            for e in all_entities
   226→            if e.get("grounding_confidence") is not None
   227→            and e["grounding_confidence"] > 0
   228→        ]
   229→
   230→        # Print actual vs baseline for visibility (pytest -s)
   231→        print(f"\n--- Regression Baseline Report for {_DEFAULT_PDF} ---")

  "content": "# Plan: Replace Static YAML Lookups with Dynamic OMOP DB Queries\n\n## Context\n\n`unit_normalizer.py` uses `ucum_mappings.yaml` (297 lines) for:\n1. **Unit normalization**: clinical unit text → UCUM code + OMOP unit_concept_id\n2. **Categorical value mapping**: qualifier words (\"positive\") → OMOP concept ID\n3. **Ordinal scale grading**: ECOG/Karnofsky/NYHA/ASA grades → OMOP concept IDs\n\nThis is brittle — misspellings, unseen aliases, or new scales break silently. The OMOP vocabulary DB (already connected via `OMOP_VOCAB_URL`) contains all this data. Replace YAML with dynamic DB queries. No YAML fallback.\n\n---\n\n## Changes\n\n### 1. Add 3 DB lookup functions to `omop_mapper.py`\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/omop_mapper.py`\n\n```python\ndef _lookup_ucum_unit(engine, unit_text: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Query concept+concept_synonym where vocabulary_id='UCUM'.\n    Returns (ucum_code, omop_concept_id) or (None, None).\"\"\"\n\ndef _lookup_value_concept(engine, value_text: str) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:\n    \"\"\"Query concept where domain_id='Meas Value' for SNOMED qualifiers.\n    Returns (normalized_text, omop_concept_id) or (None, None).\"\"\"\n\ndef _lookup_ordinal_concept(engine, scale_name: str, grade: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Query concept+concept_synonym for scale+grade combo.\n    Returns omop_concept_id or None.\"\"\"\n```\n\nAll use `_get_omop_engine()` (existing lazy singleton). All are synchronous (called from sync callers or wrapped in `asyncio.to_thread`).\n\n### 2. Rewrite `unit_normalizer.py` — DB-only, no YAML\n\n**File**: `services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py`\n\n- Delete `_load_ucum_mappings()` YAML loader\n- Delete `_load_ordinal_scales()` YAML loader\n- Delete `_CONFIG_PATH` reference to `ucum_mappings.yaml`\n- Remove `import yaml`\n\n**`normalize_unit()`**: Use `@lru_cache(maxsize=512)` wrapping `_lookup_ucum_unit()`.\n\n**`normalize_value()`**: Use `@lru_cache(maxsize=128)` wrapping `_lookup_value_concept()`.\n\n**`_match_ordinal_scale()`**: Keep as-is but use a Python dict constant instead of YAML:\n```python\n# Clinical knowledge: which names refer to which scale (not in OMOP)\nORDINAL_SCALE_ALIASES: dict[str, str] = {\n    \"ecog\": \"ecog\", \"ecog ps\": \"ecog\", \"ecog performance status\": \"ecog\",\n    \"who performance status\": \"ecog\", \"zubrod score\": \"ecog\",\n    \"karnofsky\": \"karnofsky\", \"kps\": \"karnofsky\",\n    \"karnofsky performance status\": \"karnofsky\",\n    \"nyha\": \"nyha\", \"nyha class\": \"nyha\",\n    \"nyha functional class\": \"nyha\", \"new york heart association\": \"nyha\",\n    \"asa\": \"asa\", \"asa physical status\": \"asa\", \"asa ps\": \"asa\",\n    \"asa class\": \"asa\", \"asa classification\": \"asa\", \"asa score\": \"asa\",\n}\n```\n\n**`normalize_ordinal_value()`**: Resolve grade→concept_id via `_lookup_ordinal_concept()` instead of YAML values dict. 5 grounding snippets\n    def test_grounding_snippet(self, idx, snippets, ...):\n        \"\"\"Each snippet's entities are extracted and grounded with correct codes.\"\"\"\n        snippet = snippets[idx]\n        #
